### 10.比较基因组学分析
###### **<font style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0);">比较基因组学分析的一般流程如下：</font>**[**https://mp.weixin.qq.com/s/yj3VzW6SN_AsD6_A-CF1Lw**](https://mp.weixin.qq.com/s/yj3VzW6SN_AsD6_A-CF1Lw)
1. <font style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0);">基因家族聚类分析：可以得到物种间共有基因家族和共有基因，各物种特有基因家族和特有基因及单拷贝基因家族和单拷贝基因。</font>
2. <font style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0);">进化树构建：利用获取的单拷贝基因家族，采用最大似然法，构建物种树，并借助于化石时间矫正，可以得到有分化时间的物种树，从而获得物种之间的亲缘关系和分歧时间，有利于阐明物种的进化历史。</font>
3. <font style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0);">基因家族扩张与收缩分析：通过出生死亡率模型估计每个分枝祖先的基因家族个数，从而预测出研究物种的基因家族相对于祖先的收缩和扩张情况，对扩张的基因家族进行功能富集分析，对于阐明物种特殊的功能有着重要的意义。</font>
4. <font style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0);">正选择分析。采用Branch- site model用于检测前景枝（待研究物种所在的分枝）中的受到正选择作用基因，并进行功能富集分析，对阐明物种特殊的功能有着重要的意义。</font>
5. <font style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0);">全基因组复制事件分析。主要通过Ks和4DTv方法，阐明本物种是否发生全基因组复制事件。</font>
6. <font style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0);">LTR插入时间分析。对于富含LTR重复序列的物种，可以阐明其基因组扩张的时间；通过与近缘物种比较，可以阐明基因组扩张的先后顺序。</font>

<!-- 这是一张图片，ocr 内容为： -->
![](https://cdn.nlark.com/yuque/0/2025/png/54922767/1765201644385-d9c4a54c-6cef-4fc2-ae6a-400b697fb865.png)

###### **<font style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0);">开始分析</font>**
1. <font style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0);">使用</font><font style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0);">Orthofinder</font><font style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0);">对多物种的蛋白序列进行比对，可以获得物种间所有的基因家族，并获得每个物种的单拷贝基因。</font>
2. <font style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0);">使用IQ-TREE 或raxml基于单拷贝基因构建进化树，获得物种间的进化关系。</font>
3. <font style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0);">使用paml的MCMCTREE模块计算物种间的分化时间，需要提前准备至少2组物种间的分化时间。这个分化时间可以通过</font>[https://timetree.org/](https://timetree.org/)<font style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0);"> 查询，也可以找相关的文献的支持。最后使用mcmctreeR进行可视化，绘制多物种的分化时间的进化树。</font>
4. <font style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0);">使用cafe5对基因家族扩张和收缩分析，然后使用clusterProfiler对目标物种扩张和收缩的基因分析进行GO和KEGG富集分析。</font>
5. <font style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0);">使用paml的codeML模块对筛选的几个物种进行正选择分析。</font>
6. <font style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0);">全基因组复制分析使用WGD或WGDI，此外还可以使用KaKs和4DTv的峰辅助确认物种的全基因组复制事件。首先使用jcvi找到物种间的共线性基因，然后使用KaKs_Calculator2.0计算共线性基因之间的Ka,Ks,4DTv的值。</font>
7. <font style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0);">使用LTR_retriever对多个物种的参考基因组分别进行分析，计算LTR的插入时间和峰值，以及不同物种的LTR的类别，同时还能顺便评估不同基因组的组装水平。</font>
8. <font style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0);">使用DupGen_finder 找到两个物种之间的wgd,trd,td,pd,</font><font style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0);">dsd</font><font style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0);">基因对,后续可以对不同的基因对之间进行富集分析。</font>

<font style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0);">步骤6可以使用KK4D 这个流程进行分析，步骤7可以使用LTRfind 这个流程进行分析。</font>

**<font style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0);">中间涉及到物种分化时间，物种的分子钟选择，Ks峰值换算为时间的μ，转座子的核苷酸替换率μ 这些涉及到物种分化时间的位置都需要有相关的文献或资料支持，程序默认的不一定适合你的物种</font>**

###### **<font style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0);">对于近缘物种比较的一些需求的解决方案</font>**
<font style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0);">如果你有2个亲缘关系很近的物种或者是不同品种的基因组之间相比较，适用于下面的分析思路。</font>

1. <font style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0);">找到参考基因组之间的结构变异、共线性，使用syri或AnchorWave对物种基因组进行比较，找到其中的结构变异，syri可以进行很棒的可视化。</font>
2. <font style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0);">找到不同版本基因组之间的直系同源基因，可以使用Diamond或jcvi进行比对。</font>
3. <font style="color:rgb(0, 0, 0);">现在需要构建两个不同版本的参考基因组之间的坐标转换的chain文件，以便使用liftover或crossmap转换基于不同的版本的参考基因组获得的变异文件,例如snp.vcf或indel.vcf。 可以使用minimap2或其他工具比对两个基因组，获取paf文件之后，使用paf2chain 工具把paf转为chain文件。</font>



#### <font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">OrthoFinder的分析会产生几个层次的结果：</font>
1. **<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">基因家族（Gene Families， 或称Orthogroups）</font>**<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">：这是最上层的分组。OrthoFinder根据序列相似性，将所有输入物种的所有基因分配到不同的Orthogroups中。</font>**<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">一个Orthogroup包含了一个或多个物种中，由一个共同祖先基因演化而来的所有同源基因。</font>**
2. **<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">直系同源群（Orthologues）</font>**<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">：在每个Orthogroup内部，OrthoFinder会进一步推断哪些基因对是</font>**<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">直系同源</font>**<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">（源于物种形成事件），哪些是</font>**<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">旁系同源</font>**<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">（源于基因复制事件）。</font>
3. **<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">单拷贝直系同源群（Single-Copy Orthogroups）</font>**<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">：这是在所有Orthogroups中，被筛选出来的一个</font>**<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">特殊子集</font>**<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">。要满足这个条件，必须：</font>
    - **<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">每个被分析的物种在该Orthogroup中有且仅有一个基因</font>**<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">。</font>
    - <font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">这些基因都互为直系同源关系。</font>



#### （1）直系同源基因推断OrthoFinder v3[https://github.com/OrthoFinder/OrthoFinder](https://github.com/OrthoFinder/OrthoFinder)
```bash
conda create -n of3_env python=3.10 -y
conda install orthofinder -y
conda activate of3_env

#存在的报错
#A duplicate accession was found using just first part: Pavium_014617-T2（Prunus_avium_Tieton_prot.fa）
grep "Pavium_014617-T2" Prunus_avium_prot.fa

#检查fa文件中是否有重复序列 ID
for f in *.fa; do
  echo "Checking $f ..."
  grep "^>" "$f" | sort | uniq -d > "${f}_dup.txt"
  if [ -s "${f}_dup.txt" ]; then
    echo "  --> Duplicates found in $f! See ${f}_dup.txt"
  else
    echo "  --> No duplicates in $f."
    rm "${f}_dup.txt"  # 可选：如果没有重复就删掉临时文件
  fi
done

#diamond makedb ... Error: Invalid character in sequence: '.'
#查看不合法行：
grep -v '^>' Species1.fa | grep '[^ARNDCQEGHILKMFPSTWYVX*]'
#去除非法字符（如.），如果该行以 > 开头（即注释行），则原样输出，并跳过后续处理
for file in *.fa; do
    sed '/^>/! s/\.//g' "$file" > "${file%.fa}_no_dot.fa"
done
```

###### **<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">（1）OG_Count (物种参与度)</font>**
+ **<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">统计方法</font>**<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">：对某个物种，数一列有多少个Orthogroups的值</font><font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);"> </font>**<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">>0</font>**
+ **<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">示例</font>**<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">：物种A在第1-100行都有基因（值>0），那么OG_Count=100</font>
+ **<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">含义</font>**<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">：该物种在这些基因家族中"露脸"的次数</font>

###### **<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">（2）single_OG (物种内单拷贝家族)</font>**
+ **<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">统计方法</font>**<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">：对某个物种，数一列有多少个Orthogroups的值</font><font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);"> </font>**<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">=1</font>**
+ **<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">示例</font>**<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">：物种A在第1-100行中，有60行值=1，40行值>1，那么single_OG=60</font>
+ **<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">含义</font>**<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">：该物种在这些家族中没有发生基因复制（在该物种内是单拷贝）</font>

###### **<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">（3）Global Single-Copy Orthogroups (全局单拷贝家族)</font>**
+ **<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">统计方法</font>**<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">：数整张表中有多少行，</font>**<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">所有物种列的值都 =1</font>**
+ **<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">这是生成</font>**`**<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);background-color:rgb(235, 238, 242);">Single_Copy_Orthologue_Sequences</font>**`**<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">文件夹的依据</font>**
+ **<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">条件极其苛刻</font>**<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">：要求</font>**<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">所有N个物种</font>**<font style="color:rgb(15, 17, 21);">在该家族中都恰好有1个基因</font>

```bash
#使用OrthoFinder version 2.5.5 Copyright 新服务器/data/xueqin/Prunus/genome_assembly/1Chromosome/06_polish/18.Subphaser/orthofinder2_2025.7.31

#检查是否替换成功

#在结果OrthoFinder/Results_Jul31/Orthogroups统计单拷贝基因数量
bash count_OG_per_species.sh Orthogroups.GeneCount.tsv
#count_OG_per_species.sh：
#!/bin/bash

# 输入文件检查
input_file="$1"
if [ ! -f "$input_file" ]; then
    echo "Error: input file '$input_file' not found!"
    exit 1
fi

# 获取输出文件名
name=$(basename "$input_file" .tsv)
output_file="${name}_species_OG_num.csv"

# 避免文件覆盖
nn=$(ls *.csv 2>/dev/null | grep -c "^${name}_species_OG_num")
if [ $nn -gt 0 ]; then
    output_file="${name}_${nn}_species_OG_num.csv"
    echo "$output_file already exists, saving new file as: $output_file"
fi

# 获取表头物种名
header=$(head -n 1 "$input_file")
IFS=$'\t' read -r -a species <<< "$header"

# 初始化输出
echo "Species,OG_Count,single_OG" > "$output_file"

# 遍历每个物种（从第二列开始）
num_species=${#species[@]}
for (( i=1; i<num_species; i++ )); do
    sp="${species[$i]}"
    count=$(awk -F '\t' -v col=$((i+1)) 'NR>1 && $col > 0 {c++} END{print c+0}' "$input_file")
    single=$(awk -F '\t' -v col=$((i+1)) 'NR>1 && $col == 1 {c++} END{print c+0}' "$input_file")
    echo "${sp},${count},${single}" >> "$output_file"
done

#核对结果：
awk -F'\t' '$2 == 1 {count++} END {print count}' Orthogroups.GeneCount.tsv
#7128，Amborella_trichopoda_prot

```

**/data/xueqin/Prunus/genome_assembly/1Chromosome/06_polish/18.Subphaser/orthofinder3/Protein_2025.8.5_TARI10_ANA_final/OrthoFinder/Results_Aug07/**

**分析完后，会在文件夹里面生成一个Orthofinder文件夹，里面是所有的分析结果。这个分析需要用到的单拷贝同源基因以及同源基因家族数量统计文件。**

```bash
#1.物种树构建
#物种名替换
for i in *.fa;do sed -i 's/P.zippeliana./Pzippeliana_/g;s/Phumilis/Phumilis_/g;s/persica/Ppersica_/g;s/FUN/Pavium/g;s/PaF/Psibirica_/g;s/Rosa_chinensis/Rchinensis/g;s/AmTr_/Amborella_/g;s/evm_model_/Pcampanulata_/g;s/Vitis/Vitis_/g;s/Athaliana/Athaliana_/g' $i;  done

mkdir fa_raw
mkdir fasta

#只保留物种名
for file in OG*.fa; do
    og=$(basename "$file" .fa)
    awk -F'[_ ]' '/^>/ {print $1} !/^>/' "$file" > "${og}_renamed.fa"
    mv "${og}_renamed.fa" ./fasta
done


mv *.fa ./fa_raw
#Alphabet 'U' is unknown.报错
for i in ./fasta/*.fa;do sed -i 's/U/X/g' $i; done

#mafft
mkdir mafft
for i in ./fasta/*.fa;do mafft --auto --thread 15 "$i" > "$i".mafft ; done
mv ./fasta/*.mafft ./mafft

#trimal
mkdir trimal
for gene in ./mafft/*.mafft; do
    trimal -in "$gene" -out "${gene}.tri.fasta" -gt 0.12 -terminalonly -gw 1
    mv "${gene}.tri.fasta" ./trimal
done

#iqtree2
mkdir iqtree_raw
cd trimal
for i in *.fasta;do
iqtree2 -s $i -T 15 -m MFP -bb 1000 -alrt 1000 --prefix ../iqtree_raw/$i.tre
done

cd ../
mkdir gene_tre
cd iqtree_raw
cp *.treefile ../gene_tre/

cd ../gene_tre/

mkdir ../best_rt 

#!/bin/bash

  for file in *.treefile; do
  if grep -q "Amborella" "$file"; then
    # Reroot the tree using Lyonothamnus_floribundus as the outgroup
    nw_reroot "$file" Amborella > "$file.tre"
    
    # Move the rerooted tree to the Prunus_gene_best_rt directory
    # and rename it to best.rt.tre
    mv "$file.tre" ../best_rt
    
    # Remove the original tree file
    rm "$file"
  fi
done


#Rscript reroot.R
#mv *.tre ../best_rt/

cd ../best_rt/

mkdir ../species_tree
cat *.tre> ../species_tree/sp_gene.tre
cd ../species_tree/

nw_ed sp_gene.tre 'i & b<=10' o > BS10.treefile

java -jar /home/xueqin/data/software/ASTRAL-master/Astral/astral.5.7.8.jar -i BS10.treefile -o Astral.tre

nw_reroot Astral.tre Amborella >Astral_reroot.tre
cd ../
pxcat -s ./trimal/*.fasta -p Prunus_sp_partition.txt -o Prunus_sp_supermatrix.fasta

#tree
sed -i 's/Amborella/Amborella_trichopoda/g;s/Athaliana/Arabidopsis_thaliana/g;s/Pavium/Prunus_avium/g;s/Pcampanulata/Prunus_campanulata/g;s/Phumilis/Prunus_humilis/g;s/Ppersica/Prunus_persica/g;s/Psibirica/Prunus_sibirica/g;s/Vitis/Vitis_vinifera/g;s/Pzippeliana/Prunus_zippeliana/g;s/Rchinensis/Rosa_chinensis/g' Astral_reroot.tre
#trimal
cd ../trimal
for i in *.fasta;do sed -i 's/Amborella/Amborella_trichopoda/g;s/Athaliana/Arabidopsis_thaliana/g;s/Pavium/Prunus_avium/g;s/Pcampanulata/Prunus_campanulata/g;s/Phumilis/Prunus_humilis/g;s/Ppersica/Prunus_persica/g;s/Psibirica/Prunus_sibirica/g;s/Vitis/Vitis_vinifera/g;s/Pzippeliana/Prunus_zippeliana/g;s/Rchinensis/Rosa_chinensis/g' $i;  done
```

###### **a.特有的和共有的基因**
```bash
#/data/xueqin/Prunus/genome_assembly/1Chromosome/06_polish/18.Subphaser/orthofinder3/Protein_2025.8.5_TARI10_ANA_final/OrthoFinder/Results_Aug07/Single_Copy_Orthologue_Sequences1/Unique_shared_genes_2025.8.8
mkdir Unique_shared_genes_2025.8.8
cd Unique_shared_genes_2025.8.8
cp ../../Orthogroups/Orthogroups.GeneCount.tsv ./

#步骤1：统计每个物种参与的 OG 数量（Total OGs）
awk -F '\t' '
NR==1 {
  for (i=2; i<NF; i++) header[i] = $i  # 注意 NF-1 是排除 Total 列
  next
}
{
  for (i=2; i<NF; i++) {
    if ($i > 0) count[header[i]]++
  }
}
END {
  for (sp in count) {
    print sp "\t" count[sp]
  }
}' Orthogroups.GeneCount.tsv > species_og_counts.txt

#步骤2：统计每个物种的特异OG
awk -F '\t' '
NR==1 {
  for (i=2; i<NF; i++) header[i] = $i
  next
}
{
  count = 0
  idx = -1
  for (i=2; i<NF; i++) {
    if ($i > 0) {
      count++
      idx = i
    }
  }
  if (count == 1 && idx != -1) {
    print $1 "\t" header[idx]
  }
}' Orthogroups.GeneCount.tsv > species_specific_OGs.txt

#步骤3：统计每个物种有多少个特异OG
cut -f2 species_specific_OGs.txt | sort | uniq -c | sort -nr | awk '{print $2 "\t" $1}' > species_specific_counts.txt

#步骤4：统计所有物种都拥有的 OG（core OGs）
awk -F '\t' '
NR==1 { n = NF - 2; next }  # Total列不算
{
  count = 0
  for (i=2; i<NF; i++) {
    if ($i > 0) count++
  }
  if (count == n) print $1
}' Orthogroups.GeneCount.tsv > core_OGs.txt

#步骤5：整合 og_summary.tsv 表格（所有物种、总OG数、特异OG数）
#确保两个文件都排好序：
sort species_og_counts.txt > sorted_total.txt
sort species_specific_counts.txt > sorted_specific.txt
#然后用 awk 合并：
awk '
FNR==NR { total[$1]=$2; next }
{ specific[$1]=$2 }
END {
  print "Species\tTotalOGs\tSpecificOGs"
  for (sp in total) {
    spec = (sp in specific) ? specific[sp] : 0
    print sp "\t" total[sp] "\t" spec
  }
}' sorted_total.txt sorted_specific.txt > og_summary.tsv
```

```bash
library(dplyr)
library(readr)

# 读取数据
df <- read.table("Orthogroups.GeneCount.tsv", header = TRUE, sep = "\t", check.names = FALSE)

# 去掉 Total 列（如果存在）
if ("Total" %in% colnames(df)) {
  df$Total <- NULL
}

# 所有物种列
species_cols <- setdiff(colnames(df), c("Orthogroup"))

# 目标物种
target_species <- "Prunus_zippeliana"

# 除目标物种之外的其他物种
other_species <- setdiff(species_cols, target_species)

# ---- 目标物种特有 ----
df$other_zero <- apply(df[, other_species], 1, function(x) all(x == 0))
df$target_present <- df[[target_species]] > 0
df$target_specific <- df$target_present & df$other_zero

# 特有基因家族数量
target_specific_families <- sum(df$target_specific)

# 特有基因总数
target_specific_genes <- sum(df[[target_species]][df$target_specific])

# ---- 所有物种共有基因家族 ----
df$core_og <- apply(df[, species_cols], 1, function(x) all(x > 0))
core_OG_families <- sum(df$core_og)
core_OG_genes <- sum(df[df$core_og, species_cols])

# ---- 总基因家族和总基因数 ----
total_OGs <- nrow(df)
total_genes <- sum(df[, species_cols])

# ---- 输出结果 ----
cat("===", target_species, "特有基因统计 ===\n")
cat("特有基因家族数 =", target_specific_families, "\n")
cat("特有基因总数 =", target_specific_genes, "\n\n")

cat("=== 所有物种共有基因统计 ===\n")
cat("所有物种共有基因家族数 =", core_OG_families, "\n")
cat("所有物种共有基因总数 =", core_OG_genes, "\n\n")

cat("=== 总基因家族和总基因数 ===\n")
cat("总基因家族数 =", total_OGs, "\n")
cat("总基因数 =", total_genes, "\n")

=== Prunus_zippeliana 特有基因统计 ===
特有基因家族数 = 3240
特有基因总数 = 22508

=== 所有物种共有基因统计 ===
所有物种共有基因家族数 = 9097
所有物种共有基因总数 = 174347

=== 总基因家族和总基因数 ===
总基因家族数 = 26853
总基因数 = 396342

```





###### b.拷贝情况
```bash
#/data/xueqin/Prunus/genome_assembly/1Chromosome/06_polish/18.Subphaser/orthofinder3/Protein_2025.8.5_TARI10_ANA_final/OrthoFinder/Results_Aug07/Single_Copy_Orthologue_Sequences1/Unique_shared_genes_2025.8.8
library(ggplot2)
library(readr)
library(dplyr)
library(tidyr)
# 读取TSV数据
df <- read.table("Orthogroups.GeneCount.tsv", header = TRUE, sep = "\t", check.names = FALSE)
head(df)
# 去除不需要的列
df$Total <- NULL  # 如果有Total列
orthogroups <- df$Orthogroup
df <- df[, -1]

df_long <- df %>%
  mutate(Orthogroup = orthogroups) %>%
  pivot_longer(-Orthogroup, names_to = "Species", values_to = "CopyNumber")

# 分组为五类
df_long <- df_long %>%
  mutate(CopyCategory = case_when(
    CopyNumber == 1 ~ "1",
    CopyNumber == 2 ~ "2",
    CopyNumber == 3 ~ "3",
    CopyNumber == 4 ~ "4",
    CopyNumber > 4  ~ "4+",
    CopyNumber == 0 ~ NA_character_
  )) %>%
  filter(!is.na(CopyCategory))  # 去掉 CopyNumber == 0 的情况

# 统计每个物种中各类拷贝数的基因数
df_summary <- df_long %>%
  group_by(Species, CopyCategory) %>%
  summarise(Count = n(), .groups = "drop")

# 保持拷贝类别顺序
df_summary$CopyCategory <- factor(df_summary$CopyCategory,
                                  levels = c("1", "2", "3", "4", "4+"))


# 统计每个物种中各类拷贝数的基因数
df_summary <- df_long %>%
  group_by(Species, CopyCategory) %>%
  summarise(Count = n(), .groups = "drop")

# ---- 新增：计算每个物种内部 CopyCategory 的占比 ----
df_summary <- df_summary %>%
  group_by(Species) %>%
  mutate(Total = sum(Count),
         Percentage = Count / Total) %>%
  ungroup()
df_summary
# A tibble: 50 × 5
   Species              CopyCategory Count Total Percentage
   <chr>                <chr>        <int> <int>      <dbl>
 1 Amborella_trichopoda 1            10783 14316     0.753
 2 Amborella_trichopoda 2             2101 14316     0.147
 3 Amborella_trichopoda 3              578 14316     0.0404
 4 Amborella_trichopoda 4              238 14316     0.0166
 5 Amborella_trichopoda 4+             616 14316     0.0430
 6 Arabidopsis_thaliana 1             5881 13557     0.434
 7 Arabidopsis_thaliana 2             3808 13557     0.281
 8 Arabidopsis_thaliana 3             1811 13557     0.134
 9 Arabidopsis_thaliana 4              889 13557     0.0656
10 Arabidopsis_thaliana 4+            1168 13557     0.0862

# ℹ 45 more rows
# ℹ Use `print(n = ...)` to see more rows
write.table(df_summary,
            file = "Copy_number_percentage_summary.tsv",
            sep = "\t",
            quote = FALSE,
            row.names = FALSE)
# 设定物种绘图顺序
species_order <- c(
  "Amborella_trichopoda",
  "Vitis_vinifera",
  "Arabidopsis_thaliana",
  "Rosa_chinensis",
  "Prunus_zippeliana",
  "Prunus_avium",
  "Prunus_campanulata",
  "Prunus_humilis",
  "Prunus_persica",
  "Prunus_sibirica"
)

df_summary$Species <- factor(df_summary$Species, levels = species_order)
                                  
pdf("Copy number distribution of all gene families.pdf",height = 6,width = 10)
ggplot(df_summary, aes(x = Count, y = Species, fill = CopyCategory)) +
  geom_bar(stat = "identity", position = "stack", width = 0.5) +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c(
    "1" = "#CD5C5C",
    "2" = "#354898",
    "3" = "#73ABCF",
    "4" = "#911D36",
    "4+" = "#FAAE5F"
  )) +
  labs(x = "Number of Genes (Orthogroups)", y = "Species", fill = "Copy Number") +
  theme_minimal(base_size = 12) +
  theme(
    axis.title.x = element_text(size = 16),
    axis.title.y = element_text(size = 16),
    axis.text.y = element_text(size = 12),
    axis.text.x = element_text(size = 12),
    axis.line.x = element_line(color = "black"),
    axis.line.y = element_line(color = "black"),
    panel.grid = element_blank(),
    panel.background = element_blank(),
    axis.ticks = element_line(color = "black"),
    legend.text = element_text(size = 12),
    legend.title = element_text(size = 14)
  )
dev.off()


# 计算每个物种 2 个及以上拷贝的占比
df_multi_copy <- df_summary %>%
  filter(CopyCategory %in% c("2", "3", "4", "4+")) %>%  # 选择 CopyNumber ≥ 2
  group_by(Species) %>%
  summarise(MultiCopyPercentage = sum(Percentage)) %>%
  ungroup()

df_multi_copy
```

```bash
#!/bin/bash

  for file in *.treefile; do
  if grep -q "Vitis" "$file"; then
    # Reroot the tree using Lyonothamnus_floribundus as the outgroup
    nw_reroot "$file" Vitis > "$file.tre"
    
    # Move the rerooted tree to the Prunus_gene_best_rt directory
    # and rename it to best.rt.tre
    mv "$file.tre" ../best_rt
    
    # Remove the original tree file
    rm "$file"
  fi
done


#Rscript reroot.R
#mv *.tre ../best_rt/

cd ../best_rt/

mkdir ../species_tree
cat *.tre> ../species_tree/sp_gene.tre
```

###### **a.特有的和共有的基因**
```bash
#/data/xueqin/Prunus/genome_assembly/1Chromosome/06_polish/18.Subphaser/orthofinder3/Protein_2025.8.5_TARI10final/OrthoFinder/Results_Aug07/Single_Copy_Orthologue_Sequences1/Unique_shared_genes_2025.8.8
cd Unique_shared_genes_2025.8.8
cp ../../Orthogroups/Orthogroups.GeneCount.tsv ./

#步骤1：统计每个物种参与的 OG 数量（Total OGs）
awk -F '\t' '
NR==1 {
  for (i=2; i<NF; i++) header[i] = $i  # 注意 NF-1 是排除 Total 列
  next
}
{
  for (i=2; i<NF; i++) {
    if ($i > 0) count[header[i]]++
  }
}
END {
  for (sp in count) {
    print sp "\t" count[sp]
  }
}' Orthogroups.GeneCount.tsv > species_og_counts.txt

#步骤2：统计每个物种的特异OG
awk -F '\t' '
NR==1 {
  for (i=2; i<NF; i++) header[i] = $i
  next
}
{
  count = 0
  idx = -1
  for (i=2; i<NF; i++) {
    if ($i > 0) {
      count++
      idx = i
    }
  }
  if (count == 1 && idx != -1) {
    print $1 "\t" header[idx]
  }
}' Orthogroups.GeneCount.tsv > species_specific_OGs.txt

#步骤3：统计每个物种有多少个特异OG
cut -f2 species_specific_OGs.txt | sort | uniq -c | sort -nr | awk '{print $2 "\t" $1}' > species_specific_counts.txt

#步骤4：统计所有物种都拥有的 OG（core OGs）
awk -F '\t' '
NR==1 { n = NF - 2; next }  # Total列不算
{
  count = 0
  for (i=2; i<NF; i++) {
    if ($i > 0) count++
  }
  if (count == n) print $1
}' Orthogroups.GeneCount.tsv > core_OGs.txt

#步骤5：整合 og_summary.tsv 表格（所有物种、总OG数、特异OG数）
#确保两个文件都排好序：
sort species_og_counts.txt > sorted_total.txt
sort species_specific_counts.txt > sorted_specific.txt
#然后用 awk 合并：
awk '
FNR==NR { total[$1]=$2; next }
{ specific[$1]=$2 }
END {
  print "Species\tTotalOGs\tSpecificOGs"
  for (sp in total) {
    spec = (sp in specific) ? specific[sp] : 0
    print sp "\t" total[sp] "\t" spec
  }
}' sorted_total.txt sorted_specific.txt > og_summary.tsv
```

###### b.拷贝情况
```bash
#/data/xueqin/Prunus/genome_assembly/1Chromosome/06_polish/18.Subphaser/orthofinder3/Protein_2025.8.5_TARI10_ANA_final/OrthoFinder/Results_Aug07/Single_Copy_Orthologue_Sequences1/Unique_shared_genes_2025.8.8
library(ggplot2)
library(readr)
library(dplyr)
library(tidyr)
# 读取TSV数据
df <- read.table("Orthogroups.GeneCount.tsv", header = TRUE, sep = "\t", check.names = FALSE)
head(df)
# 去除不需要的列
df$Total <- NULL  # 如果有Total列
orthogroups <- df$Orthogroup
df <- df[, -1]

df_long <- df %>%
  mutate(Orthogroup = orthogroups) %>%
  pivot_longer(-Orthogroup, names_to = "Species", values_to = "CopyNumber")

# 分组为五类
df_long <- df_long %>%
  mutate(CopyCategory = case_when(
    CopyNumber == 1 ~ "1",
    CopyNumber == 2 ~ "2",
    CopyNumber == 3 ~ "3",
    CopyNumber == 4 ~ "4",
    CopyNumber > 4  ~ "4+",
    CopyNumber == 0 ~ NA_character_
  )) %>%
  filter(!is.na(CopyCategory))  # 去掉 CopyNumber == 0 的情况

# 统计每个物种中各类拷贝数的基因数
df_summary <- df_long %>%
  group_by(Species, CopyCategory) %>%
  summarise(Count = n(), .groups = "drop")

# 保持拷贝类别顺序
df_summary$CopyCategory <- factor(df_summary$CopyCategory,
                                  levels = c("1", "2", "3", "4", "4+"))
pdf("Copy number distribution of all gene families.pdf",height = 6,width = 10)
ggplot(df_summary, aes(x = Count, y = Species, fill = CopyCategory)) +
  geom_bar(stat = "identity", position = "stack", width = 0.5) +  # 控制柱子宽度
  scale_fill_manual(values = c(
    "1" = "#CD5C5C",
    "2" = "#354898",
    "3" = "#73ABCF",
    "4" = "#911D36",
    "4+" = "#FAAE5F"
  )) +
  labs(x = "Number of Genes (Orthogroups)", y = "Species", fill = "Copy Number") +
  theme_minimal(base_size = 12) +
  theme(
    axis.title.x = element_text(size = 16),
    axis.title.y = element_text(size = 16),
    axis.text.y = element_text(size = 12),
    axis.text.x = element_text(size = 12),
    axis.line.x = element_line(color = "black"),
    axis.line.y = element_line(color = "black"),
    panel.grid = element_blank(),        # 移除所有网格线
    panel.background = element_blank(),  # 确保背景是白色
    axis.ticks = element_line(color = "black"),  # 显示刻度线（可选）
    legend.text = element_text(size = 12),
    legend.title = element_text(size = 14),
    )
dev.off()
```

###### b.拷贝情况
```bash
library(ggplot2)
library(readr)
library(dplyr)
library(tidyr)

# 读取TSV数据
df <- read.table("Orthogroups.GeneCount.tsv", header = TRUE, sep = "\t", check.names = FALSE)
head(df)

# 去除不需要的列
df$Total <- NULL  # 如果有Total列
orthogroups <- df$Orthogroup
df <- df[, -1]

df_long <- df %>%
  mutate(Orthogroup = orthogroups) %>%
  pivot_longer(-Orthogroup, names_to = "Species", values_to = "CopyNumber")

# 分组为五类
df_long <- df_long %>%
  mutate(CopyCategory = case_when(
    CopyNumber == 1 ~ "1",
    CopyNumber == 2 ~ "2",
    CopyNumber == 3 ~ "3",
    CopyNumber == 4 ~ "4",
    CopyNumber > 4  ~ "4+",
    CopyNumber == 0 ~ NA_character_
  )) %>%
  filter(!is.na(CopyCategory))  # 去掉 CopyNumber == 0 的情况

# 统计每个物种中各类拷贝数的基因数 & 占比
df_summary <- df_long %>%
  group_by(Species, CopyCategory) %>%
  summarise(Count = n(), .groups = "drop") %>%
  group_by(Species) %>%
  mutate(Proportion = Count / sum(Count) * 100) %>%
  ungroup()

# 保持拷贝类别顺序
df_summary$CopyCategory <- factor(df_summary$CopyCategory,
                                  levels = c("1", "2", "3", "4", "4+"))

# 画图（柱子调窄，width = 0.3）
pdf("Copy number distribution of all gene families.pdf", height = 6, width = 10)
ggplot(df_summary, aes(x = Count, y = Species, fill = CopyCategory)) +
  geom_bar(stat = "identity", position = "stack", width = 0.3) +  # 柱子窄一点
  scale_fill_manual(values = c(
    "1" = "#CD5C5C",
    "2" = "#354898",
    "3" = "#73ABCF",
    "4" = "#911D36",
    "4+" = "#FAAE5F"
  )) +
  labs(x = "Number of Genes (Orthogroups)", y = "Species", fill = "Copy Number") +
  theme_minimal(base_size = 12) +
  theme(
    axis.title.x = element_text(size = 16),
    axis.title.y = element_text(size = 16),
    axis.text.y = element_text(size = 12),
    axis.text.x = element_text(size = 12),
    axis.line.x = element_line(color = "black"),
    axis.line.y = element_line(color = "black"),
    panel.grid = element_blank(),
    panel.background = element_blank(),
    axis.ticks = element_line(color = "black"),
    legend.text = element_text(size = 12),
    legend.title = element_text(size = 14)
  )
dev.off()

# 查看每个物种各类拷贝数的占比表
write.csv(df_summary, "Copy_number.csv", quote = FALSE, row.names = FALSE)

# 按照 "单拷贝" 和 "多拷贝" 分组
df_summary2 <- df_summary %>%
  mutate(CopyGroup = ifelse(CopyCategory == "1", "Single", "Multi")) %>%
  group_by(Species, CopyGroup) %>%
  summarise(Count = sum(Count), .groups = "drop") %>%
  group_by(Species) %>%
  mutate(Total = sum(Count),
         Proportion = round(Count / Total * 100, 2)) %>%
  ungroup()

# 查看结果
head(df_summary2)

# 保存结果
write.csv(df_summary2, "Copy_number_Single_vs_Multi.csv", 
          quote = FALSE, row.names = FALSE)

```

#### 多拷贝基因建树
```bash
#步骤1：准备数据
# 1. 分割基因树
bash process_gene_trees.sh

#!/bin/bash
# process_gene_trees.sh

INPUT="Resolved_Gene_Trees.txt"
OUTPUT_DIR="split_gene_trees"

# 创建输出目录
mkdir -p $OUTPUT_DIR

# 方法1：按行分割（最简单）
echo "=== 方法1：按行分割 ==="
awk '{
    if ($0 ~ /;/) {
        # 如果行包含分号，认为是完整的树
        tree_num++
        filename = sprintf("'"$OUTPUT_DIR"'/OG%07d.treefile", tree_num)
        print $0 > filename
    }
}' "$INPUT"

echo "分割了 $(ls $OUTPUT_DIR/*.treefile | wc -l) 个树文件"

# 检查第一个树文件
echo -e "\n=== 第一个树文件示例 ==="
head -1 "$OUTPUT_DIR/OG0000001.treefile"

# 2. 提取物种列表
find split_gene_trees -name "*.treefile" -exec cat {} \; | \
  awk '{gsub(/[:,();]/, "\n"); print}' | \
  awk -F'_' '/^[A-Z][a-z]+_[a-z]+_/ {print $1"_"$2}' | \
  sort -u > species.txt

# 查看物种列表
cat species.txt

#步骤2：检查基因命名

# 查看几个树的tip名称
echo "=== 检查基因命名格式 ==="
for i in split_gene_trees/OG000000{1..3}.treefile; do
    echo "文件: $i"
    grep -o ">[^:]*" "$i" | head -3
    echo "---"
done

#步骤3：运行过滤脚本
假设您的基因命名已经是Species_genus_otherinfo格式：

bash
# 运行allparalogs脚本
python allparalogs_v1a.py \
  -t split_gene_trees \
  -s species.txt \
  -o filtered_paralogs \
  --min_species 11  # 14个物种的80%

# 查看结果统计
echo "=== 过滤结果 ==="
echo "总基因树数: $(ls split_gene_trees/*.treefile | wc -l)"
echo "过滤后保留: $(ls filtered_paralogs/*.treefile 2>/dev/null | wc -l)"

#步骤4：如果命名格式不符合
如果您的tip名称不是Species_genus_otherinfo格式，需要先重命名：

python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# rename_tips.py
import re
import os
import glob

def rename_tips(tree_string):
    """重命名树中的tip标签"""
    # 匹配tip名称的正则表达式
    # 例如: >Arabidopsis_thaliana_5G14950.1 → Arabidopsis_thaliana
    #       >SubADYGYChr_13g00508 → SubADYGY
    
    def replace_func(match):
        full_name = match.group(1)
        # 提取物种名
        if '_' in full_name:
            parts = full_name.split('_')
            if len(parts) >= 2:
                # 取前两部分作为物种名
                species_name = f"{parts[0]}_{parts[1]}"
            else:
                species_name = parts[0]
        else:
            # 如果没有下划线，尝试其他模式
            if full_name.startswith('Sub') and 'Chr' in full_name:
                species_name = full_name.split('Chr')[0]
            else:
                species_name = full_name.split()[0]  # 取第一个单词
        
        return species_name
    
    # 使用正则表达式替换所有tip名称
    pattern = r':([^,:()]+)(?=[,):])'
    return re.sub(pattern, lambda m: ':' + replace_func(m), tree_string)

# 处理所有树文件
input_dir = "split_gene_trees"
output_dir = "renamed_trees"
os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)

for tree_file in glob.glob(f"{input_dir}/*.treefile"):
    with open(tree_file, 'r') as f:
        tree_str = f.read().strip()
    
    # 重命名tips
    renamed_tree = rename_tips(tree_str)
    
    # 保存新文件
    out_file = os.path.join(output_dir, os.path.basename(tree_file))
    with open(out_file, 'w') as f:
        f.write(renamed_tree + "\n")
    
    print(f"Processed {os.path.basename(tree_file)}")

print(f"\n重命名完成！文件保存在 {output_dir}/")

运行重命名脚本：

bash
python rename_tips.py

# 然后使用重命名后的树
python allparalogs_v1a.py \
  -t renamed_trees \
  -s species.txt \
  -o filtered_paralogs \
  --min_species 11
五、快速测试命令
bash
# 快速测试整个流程
echo "=== 快速测试 ==="

# 1. 查看原始文件格式
echo "1. 原始文件行数:"
wc -l Resolved_Gene_Trees.txt

echo -e "\n2. 前3行内容:"
head -3 Resolved_Gene_Trees.txt

# 2. 简单分割（如果每行一个树）
awk '{
    if (NR <= 10) {  # 只处理前10个树用于测试
        print $0 > "test_trees/OG" sprintf("%07d", NR) ".treefile"
    }
}' Resolved_Gene_Trees.txt

# 3. 测试一个过滤脚本
mkdir -p test_output
python allparalogs_v1a.py \
  -t test_trees \
  -s <(echo -e "Arabidopsis_thaliana\nPrunus_mume\nSubADYGY") \
  -o test_output \
  --min_species 2

echo -e "\n测试完成！"
```















#### （2）基因家族收缩和扩张分析
学习链接：[https://mp.weixin.qq.com/s/2rsKOCdOiiwHFEXAQC1YOA](https://mp.weixin.qq.com/s/2rsKOCdOiiwHFEXAQC1YOA)、[**https://yanzhongsino.github.io/2021/10/29/bioinfo_gene.family_CAFE5/**、https://mp.weixin.qq.com/s/tSzDnDTB48s-Nxxs7hdUpQ](https://yanzhongsino.github.io/2021/10/29/bioinfo_gene.family_CAFE5/)

###### **A.无油樟基因组存在的情况**
**/data/xueqin/Prunus/genome_assembly/1Chromosome/06_polish/18.Subphaser/orthofinder3/Protein_2025.8.5_TARI10_ANA_final/OrthoFinder/Results_Aug07/**

**#OrthoFinder/Results_Aug07/Single_Copy_Orthologue_Sequences1**

**230个**

```bash
mkdir ../../CAFE_2025.8.8
cp FigTree.tre ../../CAFE_2025.8.8
cd ../../CAFE_2025.8.8
cp ../../Orthogroups/Orthogroups.GeneCount.tsv ./

#数据准备--gene_family_filter.txt和tree.txt
#1.gene_family_filter.txt
#OrthoFinder/Results_Aug01/Orthogroups/Orthogroups.GeneCount.tsv文件
#cafe.input.tsv
awk 'OFS="\t" {$NF=""; print}' Orthogroups.GeneCount.tsv > tmp && \
awk '{print "(null)" "\t" $0}' tmp > cafe.input.tsv && \
sed -i '1s/(null)/Desc/' cafe.input.tsv && \
rm tmp

#该命令行的功能是对"Orthogroups.GeneCount.tsv"文件进行处理，生成一个新的文件"cafe.input.tsv"，并在新文件的第一行将"(null)"替换为"Desc"。最后，它删除了临时文件"tmp"。
#文件gene_family_filter.txt为最终的文件，但是里面的物种必须和进化树一致
awk 'NR==1 || $3<100 && $4<100 && $5<100 && $6<100 && $7<100 && $8<100 && $9<100 && $10<100 && $11<100 && $12<100 && $13<100 && $14<100 && $15<100 && $16<100 {print $0}' cafe.input.tsv > gene_family_filter.txt 
#该命令会选择输入文件中符合指定条件（第3列到第16列的数值均小于100）的行，并将这些行输出到名为"gene_family_filter.txt"的文件中。2. 若有更多行则添加&& $Number<1003. 问什么起始是3？因为数据格式处理后前两列分别是null和OG；第三列才是数据4. 注意这里剔除大于100的值，大于100会产生假阳性
# 最后的文件格式，保证第一行的物种名字与进化树的一致即可，不能保存至桌面修改，否则一直报错。（可能我当时win整理好物种名称后，文件格式没有dos2unix转换导致）3. 在物种名称更改时，我们一开始可以先改成简称使每一行都对应一致，如下图；然后再把tree.txt的物种名改成和本文件一致即可
#或者使用
python3 /data/xueqin/software/CAFE5/docs/tutorial/clade_and_size_filter.py -i gene_family_filter.txt -o filtered_gene_families.txt -s

#2.tree.txt
#直接使用mcmctree生成的FigTree.tre文件修改一下即可使用
grep "UTREE 1 =" FigTree.tre | sed -E -e "s/\[[^]]*\]//g" -e "s/[ \t]//g" -e "/^$/d" -e "s/UTREE1=//" > tree.txt

#使用mcmctree结果作为输入的树文件：由于mcmctree默认的时间单位是100MYA，而CAFE5默认的时间单位是1MYA，因此需要修改分歧时间的数值，乘以100，可以通过编辑一个tree.nodes100.py脚本进行修改。
#3.运行CAFE5：基因家族扩张与收缩分析
#Prunus.nw.ultra.tre
library(ape)
library("phytools")
tree <- read.tree("tree.txt")
tree$edge.length <- tree$edge.length*100
tree3 <- force.ultrametric(tree)
is.ultrametric(tree3)
write.tree(tree3, "Prunus.nw.ultra.tre")
conda activate CAFE
cafe5 -i gene_family_filter.txt -t Prunus.nw.ultra.tre -p -k 2 -o k2p
#-p代表指定root frequency distribution为泊松分布（默认是均匀分布uniform distribution）。
#-k 3代表使用GAMMA模型（默认是base模型）并且使用3种gamma rate（代表不同基因家族有着不同的进化速率）。-k的值需要运行多次比较likelihood并确保收敛后才知道使用哪个最好，一般来说2-5之间试一试。
#-o 指定输出目录，默认是results
#因为报错You may want to try removing the top few families with the largest difference
#between the max and min counts and then re-run the analysis.
#Failed to initialize any reasonable values
#数据仍然包含一些极端值或不均衡分布的家族，导致 CAFE5 的 λ 参数估计失败
#自动移除差值大的家族
awk -F'\t' '{
    count = 0;
    for(i=2; i<=NF; i++) if($i > 0) count++;
    if(count >= (NF-1)*0.8) print $0;
}' gene_family_filter.txt>gene_families_filter_new.tx

#注k2p,k3p，k4p，k5p，k6p报错You may want to try removing the top few families with the largest difference
#between the max and min counts and then re-run the analysis.
#Failed to initialize any reasonable values
# 步骤 1: 过滤物种覆盖率 >= 80%
awk -F'\t' '{
    count = 0;
    for(i=2; i<=NF; i++) if($i > 0) count++;
    if(count >= (NF-1)*0.8) print $0;
}' gene_family_filter.txt > gene_families_filter_cov.txt

# 步骤 2: 移除最大最小差值大的家族（比如差值 > 50）
awk -F'\t' 'NR == 1 { print; next }
{
    min = max = $3;
    for(i = 4; i <= NF; i++) {
        if($i < min) min = $i;
        if($i > max) max = $i;
    }
    if((max - min) <= 50) print $0;
}' gene_families_filter_cov.txt > gene_families_filter_final.txt

nohup cafe5 -i gene_families_filter_final.txt -t Prunus.nw.ultra.tre -p -k 2 -o k2p &
nohup cafe5 -i gene_families_filter_final.txt -t Prunus.nw.ultra.tre -p -k 3 -o k3p &
nohup cafe5 -i gene_families_filter_final.txt -t Prunus.nw.ultra.tre -p -k 4 -o k4p &
nohup cafe5 -i gene_families_filter_final.txt -t Prunus.nw.ultra.tre -p -k 5 -o k5p &
nohup cafe5 -i gene_families_filter_final.txt -t Prunus.nw.ultra.tre -p -k 6 -o k6p &

#4.CAFE5的结果
model_asr.tre
#每个基因家族的树的合集，nexus格式
#树上的物种ID后的下划线隔开的数字是预期的基因家族大小
#树上的星号代表这个物种的基因家族有显著变化
model_clade_results.txt
#进化树上每个节点扩张或者收缩了多少基因家族
model_family_results.txt
#基因家族变化的p值和是否显著的结果
model_results.txt
#模型，最终似然值，最终Lambda值等参数信息。
选择最优结果
#k值的结果比较：选择k6p结果最佳
#查看k2p，k3p，k5p，k6p等不同的结果文件Gamma_results.txt文件中的第一行信息，Model Gamma Final Likelihood (-lnL)值，挑选最大的为最优结果。
k2p：Model Gamma Final Likelihood (-lnL): 177877
k3p：Model Gamma Final Likelihood (-lnL): 179843
k4p：Model Gamma Final Likelihood (-lnL): 178812
k5p：Model Gamma Final Likelihood (-lnL): 179948
k6p：Model Gamma Final Likelihood (-lnL): 180725

#结果整理
mkdir Prunus_zippeliana
cd Prunus_zippeliana
cp ../Gamma_change.tab ../Gamma_asr.tre ../Gamma_family_results.txt ./
#（1）对特定物种显著扩张或显著收缩基因的提取
#查看自己想获取的物种是第几列
head -1 Gamma_change.tab | tr '\t' '\n' | nl -v 0 | grep "Prunus_zippeliana"
#4  Prunus_zippeliana<4>
# 提取Gamma_change.tab第5列（Prunus_zippeliana）扩张的orthogroupsID
awk -F"\t" '$5 > 0 {print $1, $5}' Gamma_change.tab > Prunus_zippeliana.expanded

# 提取Gamma_change.tab第5列（Prunus_zippeliana）收缩的orthogroupsID
awk -F"\t" '$5 < 0 {print $1, $5}' Gamma_change.tab > Prunus_zippeliana.contracted

# 根据Prunus_zippeliana ID和编号提取其分支的显著扩张或收缩的基因家族树（p<0.05）
grep "Prunus_zippeliana<4>\*" Gamma_asr.tre > Prunus_zippeliana_significant_trees.tre

# 提取Prunus_zippeliana分支显著变化的OG IDs
grep -E -o "OG[0-9]+" Prunus_zippeliana_significant_trees.tre > Prunus_zippeliana_significant.ogs

# 以p<0.01为标准提取所有显著扩张或收缩的orthogroupsID
awk '$2 < 0.01 {print $1}' Gamma_family_results.txt > p0.01_significant.ogs

# 提取p<0.01显著性的Prunus_zippeliana分支变化的OG IDs
grep -f Prunus_zippeliana_significant.ogs p0.01_significant.ogs > Prunus_zippeliana_p0.01_significant.ogs

# 提取显著扩张的Prunus_zippeliana物种的orthogroupsID
grep -f Prunus_zippeliana_p0.01_significant.ogs Prunus_zippeliana.expanded | cut -f1 > Prunus_zippeliana.expanded.significant

# 提取显著收缩的Prunus_zippeliana物种的orthogroupsID
grep -f Prunus_zippeliana_p0.01_significant.ogs Prunus_zippeliana.contracted | cut -f1 > Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant

# 提取显著扩张的基因列表（假设基因ID前缀为P.zippeliana.）
cp ../../../../Orthogroups/Orthogroups.txt ./
cut -f1 -d' ' Prunus_zippeliana.expanded.significant | \
grep -F -f - Orthogroups.txt | \
sed 's/[ \t]/\n/g' | grep "P.zippeliana." | sort | uniq > Prunus_zippeliana.expanded.significant.genes

# 提取显著收缩的基因列表（假设基因ID前缀为bv）
cut -f1 -d' ' Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant  | \
grep -F -f - Orthogroups.txt | \
sed 's/[ \t]/\n/g' | grep "P.zippeliana." | sort | uniq > Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant.genes

cp ../../../../../../Prunus_zippeliana.fa ./
# 提取显著扩张的基因序列
seqkit grep -f Prunus_zippeliana.expanded.significant.genes Prunus_zippeliana.fa > Prunus_zippeliana.expanded.significant.pep.fa
#7065 patterns loaded from file
# 提取显著收缩的基因序列
seqkit grep -f Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant.genes Prunus_zippeliana.fa > Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant.pep.fa
#136 patterns loaded from file
#（2）提取出指定物种的显著扩张和收缩的蛋白序列之后，就可以拿去做GO注释和基因富集分析。
cp Prunus_zippeliana.expanded.significant.pep.fa expanded.significant.pep.fa
cp Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant.pep.fa contracted.significant.pep.fa
gzip contracted.significant.pep.fa
gzip expanded.significant.pep.fa


```

```bash
#饼图绘制
setwd("G:/大叶桂樱基因组原始数据/WGDI/ortofinder3_2025.7.26/modify_format_orthofinder/Prunus_mcmctree/eggnog-mapper_CAFE_ANA")

#总的占比
library(ggplot2)

# 读取 CAFE5 clade 结果文件
# 一般格式是：Clade   ExpandedFamilies  ContractedFamilies  ...
df <- read.table("Gamma_clade_results.txt", header = FALSE, sep = "\t", stringsAsFactors = FALSE)

# 手动设置列名：物种名 / 扩张数 / 收缩数
colnames(df) <- c("Species", "ExpandedFamilies", "ContractedFamilies")

head(df)
# 转为数字类型
df$ExpandedFamilies <- as.numeric(df$ExpandedFamilies)
df$ContractedFamilies <- as.numeric(df$ContractedFamilies)

total_expansion <- sum(df$ExpandedFamilies)
total_contraction <- sum(df$ContractedFamilies)

pie_data <- data.frame(
  Category = c("Expansion", "Contraction"),
  Count = c(total_expansion, total_contraction)
)

pt <- ggplot(pie_data, aes(x="", y=Count, fill=Category)) +
  geom_bar(width=1, stat="identity") +
  coord_polar(theta="y") +
  scale_fill_manual(values=c( "#4169E1","#fea443")) +
  theme_void() +
  ggtitle("Combined Change Across Lineages")

ggsave("total_expansion_contraction_pie.pdf", pt, width = 4, height = 4)


#每个节点收缩和扩张基因的占比
library(ggplot2)
library(dplyr)
library(tidyr)

# 确保数值列是 numeric
df <- df %>%
  mutate(
    ExpandedFamilies = as.numeric(ExpandedFamilies),
    ContractedFamilies = as.numeric(ContractedFamilies)
  )

# 转长格式，并给每个物种编号
df_long <- df %>%
  pivot_longer(cols = c(ExpandedFamilies, ContractedFamilies),
               names_to = "Category", values_to = "Count") %>%
  mutate(Category = ifelse(Category == "ExpandedFamilies", "Expansion", "Contraction"),
         ID = rep(1:nrow(df), each = 2)) %>%
  group_by(ID) %>%
  mutate(Count = Count / sum(Count) * 100) %>%  # 按节点归一化
  ungroup()

# 输出到 PDF
pdf("species_expansion_contraction_pies.pdf", width = 12, height = 12)
ggplot(df_long, aes(x = "", y = Count, fill = Category)) +
  geom_bar(width = 1, stat = "identity") +
  coord_polar(theta = "y") +
  facet_wrap(~ID + Species, ncol = 4) +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c("#4169E1","#fea443")) +
  theme_void() +
  theme(strip.text = element_text(size = 10)) 
  #ggtitle("Gene Family Expansion vs Contraction per Node (Proportion)")
dev.off()
#f790f7
#4d8a63

```

```bash
grep -f Prunus_zippeliana_p0.01_significant.ogs Prunus_zippeliana.expanded | cut -f1 > Prunus_zippeliana.expanded.significant

# 提取显著收缩的Prunus_zippeliana物种的orthogroupsID
grep -f Prunus_zippeliana_p0.01_significant.ogs Prunus_zippeliana.contracted | cut -f1 > Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant

# 提取显著扩张的基因列表（假设基因ID前缀为P.zippeliana.）
cp ../../../../Orthogroups/Orthogroups.txt ./
cut -f1 -d' ' Prunus_zippeliana.expanded.significant | \
grep -F -f - Orthogroups.txt | \
sed 's/[ \t]/\n/g' | grep "P.zippeliana." | sort | uniq > Prunus_zippeliana.expanded.significant.genes

# 提取显著收缩的基因列表（假设基因ID前缀为bv）
cut -f1 -d' ' Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant  | \
grep -F -f - Orthogroups.txt | \
sed 's/[ \t]/\n/g' | grep "P.zippeliana." | sort | uniq > Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant.genes

cp ../../../../../../Prunus_zippeliana.fa ./
# 提取显著扩张的基因序列
conda activate base
seqkit grep -f Prunus_zippeliana.expanded.significant.genes Prunus_zippeliana.fa > Prunus_zippeliana.expanded.significant.pep.fa
#7938 patterns loaded from file
# 提取显著收缩的基因序列
seqkit grep -f Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant.genes Prunus_zippeliana.fa > Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant.pep.fa
#214 patterns loaded from file
#（2）提取出指定物种的显著扩张和收缩的蛋白序列之后，就可以拿去做GO注释和基因富集分析。
cp Prunus_zippeliana.expanded.significant.pep.fa expanded.significant.pep.fa
cp Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant.pep.fa contracted.significant.pep.fa
gzip contracted.significant.pep.fa
gzip expanded.significant.pep.fa

```

```bash
#饼图绘制
library(ggplot2)

# 读取 CAFE5 clade 结果文件
# 一般格式是：Clade   ExpandedFamilies  ContractedFamilies  ...
df <- read.table("Gamma_clade_results.txt", header = FALSE, sep = "\t", stringsAsFactors = FALSE)

# 手动设置列名：物种名 / 扩张数 / 收缩数
colnames(df) <- c("Species", "ExpandedFamilies", "ContractedFamilies")

head(df)
# 转为数字类型
df$ExpandedFamilies <- as.numeric(df$ExpandedFamilies)
df$ContractedFamilies <- as.numeric(df$ContractedFamilies)

total_expansion <- sum(df$ExpandedFamilies)
total_contraction <- sum(df$ContractedFamilies)

pie_data <- data.frame(
  Category = c("Expansion", "Contraction"),
  Count = c(total_expansion, total_contraction)
)

pt <- ggplot(pie_data, aes(x="", y=Count, fill=Category)) +
  geom_bar(width=1, stat="identity") +
  coord_polar(theta="y") +
  scale_fill_manual(values=c( "#4169E1","#fea443")) +
  theme_void() +
  ggtitle("Combined Change Across Lineages")

ggsave("total_expansion_contraction_pie.pdf", pt, width = 4, height = 4)


#每个节点收缩和扩张基因的占比
library(ggplot2)
library(dplyr)
library(tidyr)

# 确保数值列是 numeric
df <- df %>%
  mutate(
    ExpandedFamilies = as.numeric(ExpandedFamilies),
    ContractedFamilies = as.numeric(ContractedFamilies)
  )

# 转长格式，并给每个物种编号
df_long <- df %>%
  pivot_longer(cols = c(ExpandedFamilies, ContractedFamilies),
               names_to = "Category", values_to = "Count") %>%
  mutate(Category = ifelse(Category == "ExpandedFamilies", "Expansion", "Contraction"),
         ID = rep(1:nrow(df), each = 2)) %>%
  group_by(ID) %>%
  mutate(Count = Count / sum(Count) * 100) %>%  # 按节点归一化
  ungroup()

# 输出到 PDF
pdf("species_expansion_contraction_pies.pdf", width = 12, height = 12)
ggplot(df_long, aes(x = "", y = Count, fill = Category)) +
  geom_bar(width = 1, stat = "identity") +
  coord_polar(theta = "y") +
  facet_wrap(~ID + Species, ncol = 4) +
  scale_fill_manual(values = c("#4169E1","#fea443")) +
  theme_void() +
  theme(strip.text = element_text(size = 10)) 
  #ggtitle("Gene Family Expansion vs Contraction per Node (Proportion)")
dev.off()
```

###### **C.只有李属基因组的情况**
```bash
#数据准备--gene_family_filter.txt和tree.txt
#1.gene_family_filter.txt
#OrthoFinder/Results_Aug01/Orthogroups/Orthogroups.GeneCount.tsv文件
#cafe.input.tsv
awk 'OFS="\t" {$NF=""; print}' Orthogroups.GeneCount.tsv > tmp && \
awk '{print "(null)" "\t" $0}' tmp > cafe.input.tsv && \
sed -i '1s/(null)/Desc/' cafe.input.tsv && \
rm tmp

#该命令行的功能是对"Orthogroups.GeneCount.tsv"文件进行处理，生成一个新的文件"cafe.input.tsv"，并在新文件的第一行将"(null)"替换为"Desc"。最后，它删除了临时文件"tmp"。
#文件gene_family_filter.txt为最终的文件，但是里面的物种必须和进化树一致
awk 'NR==1 || $3<100 && $4<100 && $5<100 && $6<100 && $7<100 && $8<100 && $9<100 && $10<100 && $11<100 && $12<100 && $13<100 && $14<100 && $15<100 && $16<100 {print $0}' cafe.input.tsv > gene_family_filter.txt 
#运行cafe5
nohup cafe5 -i gene_family_filter.txt -t Prunus.nw.ultra.tre -p -k 2 -o k2p &
nohup cafe5 -i gene_family_filter.txt -t Prunus.nw.ultra.tre -p -k 2 -o k2p &
nohup cafe5 -i gene_family_filter.txt -t Prunus.nw.ultra.tre -p -k 3 -o k3p &
nohup cafe5 -i gene_family_filter.txt -t Prunus.nw.ultra.tre -p -k 4 -o k4p &
nohup cafe5 -i gene_family_filter.txt -t Prunus.nw.ultra.tre -p -k 5 -o k5p &
nohup cafe5 -i gene_family_filter.txt -t Prunus.nw.ultra.tre -p -k 6 -o k6p &

#CAFE5的结果
model_results.txt
#模型，最终似然值，最终Lambda值等参数信息。
选择最优结果
#k值的结果比较：选择k6p结果最佳
#查看k2p，k3p，k5p，k6p等不同的结果文件Gamma_results.txt文件中的第一行信息，Model Gamma Final Likelihood (-lnL)值，挑选最大的为最优结果。
k2p：Model Gamma Final Likelihood (-lnL): 195492
k3p：Model Gamma Final Likelihood (-lnL): 195108
k4p：Model Gamma Final Likelihood (-lnL): 194949
k5p：Model Gamma Final Likelihood (-lnL): 194905
k6p：Model Gamma Final Likelihood (-lnL): 196430

#结果整理
cd k6p
mkdir Prunus_zippeliana
cd Prunus_zippeliana
cp ../Gamma_change.tab ../Gamma_asr.tre ../Gamma_family_results.txt ./
#（1）对特定物种显著扩张或显著收缩基因的提取
#查看自己想获取的物种是第几列
head -1 Gamma_change.tab | tr '\t' '\n' | nl -v 0 | grep "Prunus_zippeliana"
# 3  Prunus_zippeliana<3>

# 提取Gamma_change.tab第4列（Prunus_zippeliana）扩张的orthogroupsID
awk -F"\t" '$4 > 0 {print $1, $4}' Gamma_change.tab > Prunus_zippeliana.expanded

# 提取Gamma_change.tab第6列（Prunus_zippeliana）收缩的orthogroupsID
awk -F"\t" '$4 < 0 {print $1, $4}' Gamma_change.tab > Prunus_zippeliana.contracted

# 根据Prunus_zippeliana ID和编号提取其分支的显著扩张或收缩的基因家族树（p<0.05）
grep "Prunus_zippeliana<1>\*" Gamma_asr.tre > Prunus_zippeliana_significant_trees.tre

# 提取Prunus_zippeliana分支显著变化的OG IDs
grep -E -o "OG[0-9]+" Prunus_zippeliana_significant_trees.tre > Prunus_zippeliana_significant.ogs

# 以p<0.01为标准提取所有显著扩张或收缩的orthogroupsID
awk '$2 < 0.01 {print $1}' Gamma_family_results.txt > p0.01_significant.ogs

# 提取p<0.01显著性的Prunus_zippeliana分支变化的OG IDs
grep -f Prunus_zippeliana_significant.ogs p0.01_significant.ogs > Prunus_zippeliana_p0.01_significant.ogs

# 提取显著扩张的Prunus_zippeliana物种的orthogroupsID
grep -f Prunus_zippeliana_p0.01_significant.ogs Prunus_zippeliana.expanded | cut -f1 > Prunus_zippeliana.expanded.significant

# 提取显著收缩的Prunus_zippeliana物种的orthogroupsID
grep -f Prunus_zippeliana_p0.01_significant.ogs Prunus_zippeliana.contracted | cut -f1 > Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant

# 提取显著扩张的基因列表（假设基因ID前缀为P.zippeliana.）
cp ../../../../Orthogroups/Orthogroups.txt ./
cut -f1 -d' ' Prunus_zippeliana.expanded.significant | \
grep -F -f - Orthogroups.txt | \
sed 's/[ \t]/\n/g' | grep "P.zippeliana." | sort | uniq > Prunus_zippeliana.expanded.significant.genes

# 提取显著收缩的基因列表（假设基因ID前缀为bv）
cut -f1 -d' ' Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant  | \
grep -F -f - Orthogroups.txt | \
sed 's/[ \t]/\n/g' | grep "P.zippeliana." | sort | uniq > Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant.genes

cp ../../../../../../Prunus_zippeliana.fa ./
# 提取显著扩张的基因序列
seqkit grep -f Prunus_zippeliana.expanded.significant.genes Prunus_zippeliana.fa > Prunus_zippeliana.expanded.significant.pep.fa
#[INFO] 213 patterns loaded from file
# 提取显著收缩的基因序列
seqkit grep -f Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant.genes Prunus_zippeliana.fa > Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant.pep.fa
#[INFO] 3665 patterns loaded from file
#（2）提取出指定物种的显著扩张和收缩的蛋白序列之后，就可以拿去做GO注释和基因富集分析。
cp Prunus_zippeliana.expanded.significant.pep.fa expanded.significant.pep.fa
cp Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant.pep.fa contracted.significant.pep.fa
gzip contracted.significant.pep.fa
gzip expanded.significant.pep.fa

#5.把每个节点收缩扩张的基因数量画在树上
#绘制基因家族扩张数量的图片：
python3 /data/xueqin/software/CAFE5/docs/tutorial/cafe5_draw_tree.py \
  -i Gamma_clade_results.txt \
  -d Gamma_report.cafe \
  -y Increase \
  -o expansion_tree.pdf
  
#绘制基因家族收缩数量的图片：
python3 /data/xueqin/software/CAFE5/docs/tutorial/cafe5_draw_tree.py \

#整理进化树格式画图即可
awk '/TREE \S+ =/{print $NF}' Gamma_asr.tre | sed -r 's/_[0-9]+//g' | sed -r 's/([^)])<[0-9]+>/\1/g' | sed 's/*//g' > out_ortho.tree
#最后上传到ITOL美化即可
```

#### （3）特异基因家族富集
###### **A.无油樟基因组存在的情况:TBtools的 GO富集分析或者KEGG富集分析**
###### **GO富集分析**
```bash
#学习链接（https://zhuanlan.zhihu.com/p/22933626991、https://www.yuque.com/cjchen/hirv8i/pohup4、https://www.yuque.com/cjchen/hirv8i/nwgiwgzqv5mk0gtw）
#我下载的KEGG和GO富集需要从网上下载的数据，
#放置于G:\大叶桂樱基因组原始数据\WGDI\KEGG_enrichment_2025.7.11\TBtools.TBtoolsKeggBackEnd

#GO富集分析
需要准备三个文件：GO的层级结构文件，即go-basic.obo，可直接从网站上下载，点击摁钮即可跳转；
GO注释的背景信息，query2Go.backGround，这个文件一般需要先通过GO注释获得；
#G:\大叶桂樱基因组原始数据\WGDI\ortofinder3_2025.7.26\modify_format_orthofinder\Prunus_mcmctree\eggnog-mapper_CAFE_ANA
sed 's/,/\t/g' contracted_GO_all.csv>contracted_GO_all.txt
选择集，如差异表达基因的ID列表，或者通过韦恩图分析获得的ID列表；

```

**#G:\大叶桂樱基因组原始数据\WGDI\ortofinder3_2025.7.26\modify_format_orthofinder\Prunus_mcmctree\eggnog-mapper_CAFE_ANA\old_8.5_拟南芥序列出问题\Prunus_zippeliana.all.annotations.GO.txt为GO的背景文件**

**#结果：contracted.TBtools5870370190162529393.enrich.temp.GO.Enrichment.final.xls**

<!-- 这是一张图片，ocr 内容为： -->
![](https://cdn.nlark.com/yuque/0/2025/png/54922767/1754648938159-4cbbcf48-0a30-4140-bbe3-be32bbfc1e74.png)

<!-- 这是一张图片，ocr 内容为： -->

```bash
library(data.table)
library(dplyr)
library(tidyr)
library(clusterProfiler)
library(ggplot2)
library(GO.db)
library(AnnotationDbi)

#====================#
# 1. 读取 emapper 注释
#====================#
go2gene <- fread("G:/大叶桂樱基因组原始数据/WGDI/ortofinder3_2025.7.26/modify_format_orthofinder/Prunus_mcmctree/eggnog-mapper_CAFE_only_Prunus/DYGY.emapper.clean.tsv", sep="\t", header=TRUE, stringsAsFactors=F)

# 展开 GOs 列（多个GO用逗号分隔）
gene2go <- go2gene %>%
  filter(GOs != "-" & GOs != "") %>%
  separate_rows(GOs, sep = ",") %>%
  dplyr::select(`#query`, GOs)

colnames(gene2go) <- c("GeneID","GO")

#====================#
# 2. 构建 TERM2GENE 和 TERM2NAME
#====================#
go_annot <- gene2go %>%
  dplyr::select(GO, GeneID)

# 用 GO.db 提取 GO 号 → 名称
go2name <- AnnotationDbi::select(
  GO.db, 
  keys=unique(gene2go$GO), 
  columns=c("TERM"), 
  keytype="GOID"
)
colnames(go2name) <- c("GO","Description")

# 背景基因
bg_genes <- unique(gene2go$GeneID)

#====================#
# 3. 差异基因列表
#====================#
setwd("G:/大叶桂樱基因组原始数据/WGDI/ortofinder3_2025.7.26/modify_format_orthofinder/Prunus_mcmctree/eggnog-mapper_CAFE_ANA/")
gene_list <- read.table("Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant.genes", header=FALSE)
colnames(gene_list) <- "GeneID"
gene_ids <- as.character(gene_list$GeneID)

# 取交集
intersect_genes <- intersect(gene_ids, bg_genes)
length(intersect_genes)

#====================#
# 4. GO 富集分析
#====================#
ego <- enricher(
  gene          = intersect_genes,
  universe      = bg_genes,
  TERM2GENE     = go_annot,
  TERM2NAME     = go2name,
  pvalueCutoff  = 0.05,
  qvalueCutoff  = 0.05
)

#====================#
# 5. 查看结果
#====================#
head(ego@result)

# 可视化
pdf("DYGY.contracted.significant.genes.GO.pdf",height = 16,width = 12)
dotplot(ego, showCategory=20) + ggtitle("GO Enrichment (contracted genes)")
dev.off()

barplot(ego, showCategory=20) + ggtitle("GO Enrichment (contracted genes)")


# 所有富集 GO ID
all_go_ids <- ego@result$ID

# 查询 GO 类型（BP / MF / CC）
go_type <- AnnotationDbi::select(
  GO.db,
  keys = all_go_ids,
  columns = c("ONTOLOGY"),
  keytype = "GOID"
)

# 合并类型信息到富集结果表
ego_result_with_type <- ego@result %>%
  left_join(go_type, by = c("ID" = "GOID"))

# 查看前几行
head(ego_result_with_type)

# 保存到 CSV
write.csv(ego_result_with_type, "contracted_GO_enrichment_all_with_type.csv", row.names = FALSE)


```

```bash
# 展开 GOs 列（多个GO用逗号分隔）
gene2go <- go2gene %>%
  filter(GOs != "-" & GOs != "") %>%

colnames(gene2go) <- c("GeneID","GO")

#====================#
# 2. 构建 TERM2GENE 和 TERM2NAME
#====================#

  dplyr::select(GO, GeneID)

  GO.db,

  columns=c("TERM"), 
  keytype="GOID"
)
colnames(go2name) <- c("GO","Description")

# 背景基因
bg_genes <- unique(gene2go$GeneID)

#====================#
# 3. 差异基因列表
#====================#
setwd("G:/大叶桂樱基因组原始数据/WGDI/ortofinder3_2025.7.26/modify_format_orthofinder/Prunus_mcmctree/eggnog-mapper_CAFE_ANA/")
gene_list <- read.table("Prunus_zippeliana.expanded.significant.genes", header=FALSE)
colnames(gene_list) <- "GeneID"
gene_ids <- as.character(gene_list$GeneID)

# 取交集
intersect_genes <- intersect(gene_ids, bg_genes)
length(intersect_genes)

#====================#
# 4. GO 富集分析
#====================#
ego <- enricher(
  gene          = intersect_genes,
  universe      = bg_genes,
  TERM2GENE     = go_annot,
  TERM2NAME     = go2name,
  pvalueCutoff  = 0.05,
  qvalueCutoff  = 0.05
)

#====================#
# 5. 查看结果
#====================#
head(ego@result)

# 可视化
pdf("DYGY.expanded.significant.genes.GO.pdf",height = 16,width = 12)
dotplot(ego, showCategory=20) + ggtitle("GO Enrichment (expanded genes)")
dev.off()

barplot(ego, showCategory=20) + ggtitle("GO Enrichment (expanded genes)")


# 所有富集 GO ID
all_go_ids <- ego@result$ID

# 查询 GO 类型（BP / MF / CC）

  GO.db,

  columns = c("ONTOLOGY"),
  keytype = "GOID"
)

# 合并类型信息到富集结果表

  left_join(go_type, by = c("ID" = "GOID"))
```

###### **KEGG富集分析**
**#G:\大叶桂樱基因组原始数据\WGDI\ortofinder3_2025.7.26\modify_format_orthofinder\Prunus_mcmctree\eggnog-mapper_CAFE_ANA\old_8.5_拟南芥序列出问题\Prunus_zippeliana.all.annotations.KEGG_Knum.txt为KEGG的背景文件**

<!-- 这是一张图片，ocr 内容为： -->
![](https://cdn.nlark.com/yuque/0/2025/png/54922767/1754649132746-80c1fdbe-ee00-48fc-b1b6-d0c4c01e0bd8.png)

**expanded.TBtools.KEGG.enrichment.result.xls.final.xls**

**expanded.significant.KEGG_top30.pdf**

<!-- 这是一张图片，ocr 内容为： -->
![](https://cdn.nlark.com/yuque/0/2025/png/54922767/1754649439911-e1b87208-f1c3-4ce1-bad1-83a70ef2c880.png)

```bash
# ------------------------------
# 加载包
library(dplyr)
library(tidyr)
library(readr)
library(clusterProfiler)
library(enrichplot)
library(ggplot2)

# ------------------------------
# 1. 读取 KO 功能信息表
ko2func <- read.csv("G:/大叶桂樱基因组原始数据/WGDI/ortofinder3_2025.7.26/modify_format_orthofinder/Prunus_mcmctree/eggnog-mapper_CAFE_only_Prunus/KEGG_KO_function_table.csv", stringsAsFactors = FALSE)

# 保留每个 KO_ID 的唯一功能信息
ko2func_unique <- ko2func %>%
  filter(KO_ID != "-") %>%
  group_by(KO_ID) %>%
  summarise(
    Function = dplyr::first(Function),
    Level1_Pathway = dplyr::first(Level1_Pathway),
    Level2_Pathway = dplyr::first(Level2_Pathway),
    Level3_Pathway = dplyr::first(Level3_Pathway),
    .groups = "drop"
  )

# ------------------------------
# 2. 读取 KEGG 注释文件 (GeneID + KO)
kegg_anno <- read_tsv("G:/大叶桂樱基因组原始数据/WGDI/ortofinder3_2025.7.26/modify_format_orthofinder/Prunus_mcmctree/eggnog-mapper_CAFE_only_Prunus/DYGY.emapper.clean.tsv", col_types = cols(.default = "c"))

term2gene <- kegg_anno[, c(1, 12)]
colnames(term2gene) <- c("gene", "KO")
term2gene <- term2gene %>% filter(KO != "-")
term2gene_clean <- term2gene %>% separate_rows(KO, sep = ",")
TERM2GENE <- data.frame(
  gene = term2gene_clean$gene,
  term = term2gene_clean$KO,
  stringsAsFactors = FALSE
)

# 去掉 ko: 前缀
TERM2GENE <- TERM2GENE %>% mutate(KO_ID = sub("^ko:", "", term))

# ------------------------------
# 3. 保存基因对应功能信息（可选）
gene_kegg_func <- TERM2GENE %>%
  left_join(ko2func_unique, by = "KO_ID") %>%
  dplyr::select(gene, KO = term, Function, Level1_Pathway, Level2_Pathway, Level3_Pathway)
write.csv(gene_kegg_func, "gene_KO_function.csv", row.names = FALSE)


setwd("G:/大叶桂樱基因组原始数据/WGDI/ortofinder3_2025.7.26/modify_format_orthofinder/Prunus_mcmctree/eggnog-mapper_CAFE_ANA/")
# ------------------------------
# 4. 读取差异基因列表
gene_list <- read.table("Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant.genes",
                        header = FALSE, stringsAsFactors = FALSE)[,1]

head(gene_list)
# 背景基因集
bg_genes <- unique(TERM2GENE$gene)

# ------------------------------
# 5. KEGG 富集分析
TERM2GENE_for_enricher <- TERM2GENE %>% dplyr::select(KO_ID, gene) %>% dplyr::rename(term = KO_ID)

ego_kegg <- enricher(
  gene      = gene_list,
  universe  = bg_genes,
  TERM2GENE = TERM2GENE_for_enricher,
  pAdjustMethod = "BH",
  pvalueCutoff  = 0.05,
  qvalueCutoff  = 0.05
)

# ------------------------------
# 6. 添加功能描述
ego_kegg_df <- ego_kegg@result %>%
  left_join(ko2func_unique %>% dplyr::select(KO_ID, Function), by = c("ID" = "KO_ID")) %>%
  mutate(Description = ifelse(is.na(Function), ID, Function))

write.csv(ego_kegg_df, "Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant.genes_KEGG.csv", row.names = FALSE)

# ------------------------------
# 7. 给 ego_kegg 对象加上 Description 列（功能名）
ego_kegg@result$Description <- ego_kegg_df$Description

# ------------------------------
# 8. 可视化 dotplot，显示功能名称
pdf("Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant.genes_KEGG.pdf", height = 16, width = 12)
dotplot(ego_kegg, showCategory = 20, label_format = 60) +
  ggtitle("KEGG Enrichment Dotplot (contracted genes)") +
  scale_y_discrete(labels = ego_kegg@result$Description)
dev.off()

# ------------------------------
# 输出所有 KEGG 富集功能及路径信息
ego_kegg_full <- ego_kegg@result %>%
  left_join(ko2func_unique %>% dplyr::select(KO_ID, Function, Level1_Pathway, Level2_Pathway, Level3_Pathway),
            by = c("ID" = "KO_ID")) %>%
  dplyr::select(ID, Count, pvalue, p.adjust, Function, Level1_Pathway, Level2_Pathway, Level3_Pathway)

# 保存为 CSV
write.csv(ego_kegg_full, "Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant.genes_KEGG_with_pathway.csv", row.names = FALSE)


```

```bash
#学习链接（https://zhuanlan.zhihu.com/p/22933626991、https://www.yuque.com/cjchen/hirv8i/pohup4、https://www.yuque.com/cjchen/hirv8i/nwgiwgzqv5mk0gtw）
#我下载的KEGG和GO富集需要从网上下载的数据，

#GO富集分析
需要准备三个文件：GO的层级结构文件，即go-basic.obo，可直接从网站上下载，点击摁钮即可跳转；
GO注释的背景信息，query2Go.backGround，这个文件一般需要先通过GO注释获得；
#G:\大叶桂樱基因组原始数据\WGDI\ortofinder3_2025.7.26\modify_format_orthofinder\Prunus_mcmctree\eggnog-mapper_CAFE_ANA
sed 's/,/\t/g' contracted_GO_all.csv>contracted_GO_all.txt
选择集，如差异表达基因的ID列表，或者通过韦恩图分析获得的ID列表；
```

###### **C.只有李属基因存在的情况:TBtools的 GO富集分析或者KEGG富集分析**
```bash
seqkit split2 -p 2 Prunus_zippeliana.fa
gzip Prunus_zippeliana.fa.split/Prunus_zippeliana.part_001.fa
gzip Prunus_zippeliana.fa.split/Prunus_zippeliana.part_002.fa

#将压缩包分别输入http://eggnog-mapper.embl.de/
grep -v "^##" part_002.fa.out.emapper.annotations > part_002.fa.emapper.clean.tsv
grep -v "^##" part_001.fa.out.emapper.annotations > part_001.fa.emapper.clean.tsv

cat part_001.fa.emapper.clean.tsv <(awk 'NR>1' part_002.fa.emapper.clean.tsv) > DYGY.emapper.clean.tsv

#/data/xueqin/Prunus/genome_assembly/1Chromosome/06_polish/20.DupGen-finder/DupGen-finder/outgroup_Amborella/DYGY
step1.Parse.KEGG.py
step2.drop.pathway.KEGG.py
step3.KEGG_pathway_ko_uniq.py
#最终获得
KEGG_KO_function_table.csv
KEGG_KO_function_table.xlsx

```

**李属收缩和扩张的基因**

| Job name | User | Date created | File | Sequences (type) | Total length | Min. hit e-value | Min. hit bit-score | Min. % of identity | Min. % of query cov. | Min. % of subject cov. | Tax. scope | Orth. restrictions | GO evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

###### a. contracted 收缩基因
###### **GO富集分析**
```bash
library(data.table)
library(dplyr)
library(tidyr)
library(clusterProfiler)
library(ggplot2)
library(GO.db)
library(AnnotationDbi)

#====================#
# 1. 读取 emapper 注释
#====================#
go2gene <- fread("DYGY.emapper.clean.tsv", sep="\t", header=TRUE, stringsAsFactors=F)

# 展开 GOs 列（多个GO用逗号分隔）
gene2go <- go2gene %>%
  filter(GOs != "-" & GOs != "") %>%
  separate_rows(GOs, sep = ",") %>%
  dplyr::select(`#query`, GOs)

colnames(gene2go) <- c("GeneID","GO")

#====================#
# 2. 构建 TERM2GENE 和 TERM2NAME
#====================#
go_annot <- gene2go %>%
  dplyr::select(GO, GeneID)

# 用 GO.db 提取 GO 号 → 名称
go2name <- AnnotationDbi::select(
  GO.db, 
  keys=unique(gene2go$GO), 
  columns=c("TERM"), 
  keytype="GOID"
)
colnames(go2name) <- c("GO","Description")

# 背景基因
bg_genes <- unique(gene2go$GeneID)

#====================#
# 3. 差异基因列表
#====================#
gene_list <- read.table("Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant.genes", header=FALSE)
colnames(gene_list) <- "GeneID"
gene_ids <- as.character(gene_list$GeneID)

# 取交集
intersect_genes <- intersect(gene_ids, bg_genes)
length(intersect_genes)

#====================#
# 4. GO 富集分析
#====================#
ego <- enricher(
  gene          = intersect_genes,
  universe      = bg_genes,
  TERM2GENE     = go_annot,
  TERM2NAME     = go2name,
  pvalueCutoff  = 0.05,
  qvalueCutoff  = 0.05
)

#====================#
# 5. 查看结果
#====================#
head(ego@result)

# 可视化
pdf("DYGY.contracted.significant.genes.GO.pdf",height = 16,width = 12)
dotplot(ego, showCategory=20) + ggtitle("GO Enrichment (Contracted genes)")
dev.off()

barplot(ego, showCategory=20) + ggtitle("GO Enrichment (Contracted genes)")


# 所有富集 GO ID
all_go_ids <- ego@result$ID

# 查询 GO 类型（BP / MF / CC）
go_type <- AnnotationDbi::select(
  GO.db,
  keys = all_go_ids,
  columns = c("ONTOLOGY"),
  keytype = "GOID"
)

# 合并类型信息到富集结果表
ego_result_with_type <- ego@result %>%
  left_join(go_type, by = c("ID" = "GOID"))

# 查看前几行
head(ego_result_with_type)

# 保存到 CSV
write.csv(ego_result_with_type, "GO_enrichment_all_with_type.csv", row.names = FALSE)


```

###### **KEGG富集分析**
```bash
# ------------------------------
# 加载包
library(dplyr)
library(tidyr)
library(readr)
library(clusterProfiler)
library(enrichplot)
library(ggplot2)

# ------------------------------
# 1. 读取 KO 功能信息表
ko2func <- read.csv("KEGG_KO_function_table.csv", stringsAsFactors = FALSE)

# 保留每个 KO_ID 的唯一功能信息
ko2func_unique <- ko2func %>%
  filter(KO_ID != "-") %>%
  group_by(KO_ID) %>%
  summarise(
    Function = dplyr::first(Function),
    Level1_Pathway = dplyr::first(Level1_Pathway),
    Level2_Pathway = dplyr::first(Level2_Pathway),
    Level3_Pathway = dplyr::first(Level3_Pathway),
    .groups = "drop"
  )

# ------------------------------
# 2. 读取 KEGG 注释文件 (GeneID + KO)
kegg_anno <- read_tsv("DYGY.emapper.clean.tsv", col_types = cols(.default = "c"))

term2gene <- kegg_anno[, c(1, 12)]
colnames(term2gene) <- c("gene", "KO")
term2gene <- term2gene %>% filter(KO != "-")
term2gene_clean <- term2gene %>% separate_rows(KO, sep = ",")
TERM2GENE <- data.frame(
  gene = term2gene_clean$gene,
  term = term2gene_clean$KO,
  stringsAsFactors = FALSE
)

# 去掉 ko: 前缀
TERM2GENE <- TERM2GENE %>% mutate(KO_ID = sub("^ko:", "", term))

# ------------------------------
# 3. 保存基因对应功能信息（可选）
gene_kegg_func <- TERM2GENE %>%
  left_join(ko2func_unique, by = "KO_ID") %>%
  dplyr::select(gene, KO = term, Function, Level1_Pathway, Level2_Pathway, Level3_Pathway)
write.csv(gene_kegg_func, "gene_KO_function.csv", row.names = FALSE)

# ------------------------------
# 4. 读取差异基因列表
gene_list <- read.table("Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant.genes",
                        header = FALSE, stringsAsFactors = FALSE)[,1]

head(gene_list)
# 背景基因集
bg_genes <- unique(TERM2GENE$gene)

# ------------------------------
# 5. KEGG 富集分析
TERM2GENE_for_enricher <- TERM2GENE %>% dplyr::select(KO_ID, gene) %>% dplyr::rename(term = KO_ID)

ego_kegg <- enricher(
  gene      = gene_list,
  universe  = bg_genes,
  TERM2GENE = TERM2GENE_for_enricher,
  pAdjustMethod = "BH",
  pvalueCutoff  = 0.05,
  qvalueCutoff  = 0.05
)

# ------------------------------
# 6. 添加功能描述
ego_kegg_df <- ego_kegg@result %>%
  left_join(ko2func_unique %>% dplyr::select(KO_ID, Function), by = c("ID" = "KO_ID")) %>%
  mutate(Description = ifelse(is.na(Function), ID, Function))

write.csv(ego_kegg_df, "Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant.genes_KEGG.csv", row.names = FALSE)

# ------------------------------
# 7. 给 ego_kegg 对象加上 Description 列（功能名）
ego_kegg@result$Description <- ego_kegg_df$Description

# ------------------------------
# 8. 可视化 dotplot，显示功能名称
pdf("Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant.genes_KEGG.pdf", height = 16, width = 12)
dotplot(ego_kegg, showCategory = 20, label_format = 60) +
  ggtitle("KEGG Enrichment Dotplot (contracted genes)") +
  scale_y_discrete(labels = ego_kegg@result$Description)
dev.off()

# ------------------------------
# 输出所有 KEGG 富集功能及路径信息
ego_kegg_full <- ego_kegg@result %>%
  left_join(ko2func_unique %>% dplyr::select(KO_ID, Function, Level1_Pathway, Level2_Pathway, Level3_Pathway),
            by = c("ID" = "KO_ID")) %>%
  dplyr::select(ID, Count, pvalue, p.adjust, Function, Level1_Pathway, Level2_Pathway, Level3_Pathway)

# 保存为 CSV
write.csv(ego_kegg_full, "Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant.genes_KEGG_with_pathway.csv", row.names = FALSE)

```

###### b. significant 扩张基因
###### **GO富集分析**
```bash
library(data.table)
library(dplyr)
library(tidyr)
library(clusterProfiler)
library(ggplot2)
library(GO.db)
library(AnnotationDbi)

#====================#
# 1. 读取 emapper 注释
#====================#
go2gene <- fread("DYGY.emapper.clean.tsv", sep="\t", header=TRUE, stringsAsFactors=F)

# 展开 GOs 列（多个GO用逗号分隔）
gene2go <- go2gene %>%
  filter(GOs != "-" & GOs != "") %>%
  separate_rows(GOs, sep = ",") %>%
  dplyr::select(`#query`, GOs)

colnames(gene2go) <- c("GeneID","GO")

#====================#
# 2. 构建 TERM2GENE 和 TERM2NAME
#====================#
go_annot <- gene2go %>%
  dplyr::select(GO, GeneID)

# 用 GO.db 提取 GO 号 → 名称
go2name <- AnnotationDbi::select(
  GO.db, 
  keys=unique(gene2go$GO), 
  columns=c("TERM"), 
  keytype="GOID"
)
colnames(go2name) <- c("GO","Description")

# 背景基因
bg_genes <- unique(gene2go$GeneID)

#====================#
# 3. 差异基因列表
#====================#
gene_list <- read.table("Prunus_zippeliana.expanded.significant.genes", header=FALSE)
colnames(gene_list) <- "GeneID"
gene_ids <- as.character(gene_list$GeneID)

# 取交集
intersect_genes <- intersect(gene_ids, bg_genes)
length(intersect_genes)

#====================#
# 4. GO 富集分析
#====================#
ego <- enricher(
  gene          = intersect_genes,
  universe      = bg_genes,
  TERM2GENE     = go_annot,
  TERM2NAME     = go2name,
  pvalueCutoff  = 0.05,
  qvalueCutoff  = 0.05
)

#====================#
# 5. 查看结果
#====================#
head(ego@result)

# 可视化
pdf("Prunus_zippeliana.expanded.significant.genes.GO.pdf",height = 16,width = 12)
dotplot(ego, showCategory=20) + ggtitle("GO Enrichment (expanded genes)")
dev.off()

barplot(ego, showCategory=20) + ggtitle("GO Enrichment (expanded genes)")

#====================#
# 6. 输出GO富集类型
#====================#

# 所有富集 GO ID
all_go_ids <- ego@result$ID

# 查询 GO 类型（BP / MF / CC）
go_type <- AnnotationDbi::select(
  GO.db,
  keys = all_go_ids,
  columns = c("ONTOLOGY"),
  keytype = "GOID"
)

# 合并类型信息到富集结果表
ego_result_with_type <- ego@result %>%
  left_join(go_type, by = c("ID" = "GOID"))

# 查看前几行
head(ego_result_with_type)

# 保存到 CSV
write.csv(ego_result_with_type, "Prunus_zippeliana.expanded.significant.genes.GO_with_type.csv", row.names = FALSE)

```

```bash
#
for file in OG*.fa; do
    og=$(basename "$file" .fa)
    
    # 创建调试文件查看匹配情况
    echo "Processing $og" >&2
    
    awk '/^>/ {
        original = $0
        
        # 模式1: Arabidopsis_thaliana_5G14950.1
        if (match($0, /^>([A-Z][a-z]+_[a-z]+)_/)) {
            species = substr($0, RSTART+1, RLENGTH-2)
            print ">" species
            print "DEBUG: " original " -> " species > "/dev/stderr"
        }
        # 模式2: SubADYGYChr_13g00508  
        else if (match($0, /^>(Sub[A-Z]+)Chr_/)) {
            species = substr($0, RSTART+1, RLENGTH-4)
            print ">" species
            print "DEBUG: " original " -> " species > "/dev/stderr"
        }
        # 模式3: 通用处理（两个下划线的情况）
        else if (gsub(/_/, "_") >= 2) {
            split($0, parts, "_")
            print ">" parts[1] "_" parts[2]
            print "DEBUG: " original " -> " parts[1] "_" parts[2] > "/dev/stderr"
        }
        # 默认：直接取>后的内容直到第一个数字或特殊字符
        else {
            if (match($0, /^>([A-Za-z_]+)/)) {
                species = substr($0, RSTART+1, RLENGTH-1)
                print ">" species
                print "DEBUG: " original " -> " species > "/dev/stderr"
            } else {
                print original
            }
        }
        next
    }
    
    !/^>/ {print}
    
    ' "$file" > "${og}_renamed.fa"
    
    mv "${og}_renamed.fa" ../fasta
done
```

```bash
library(dplyr)
library(readr)

# 读取数据
df <- read.table("Orthogroups.GeneCount.tsv", header = TRUE, sep = "\t", check.names = FALSE)

# 去掉 Total 列（如果存在）
if ("Total" %in% colnames(df)) {
  df$Total <- NULL
}

# 所有物种列
species_cols <- setdiff(colnames(df), c("Orthogroup"))

# 除了 SubA/SubB 之外的其他物种
other_species <- setdiff(species_cols, c("DYGY_SubA", "DYGY_SubB"))

# ---- DYGY_SubA 特有 ----
df$other_zero_A <- apply(df[, other_species], 1, function(x) all(x == 0))
df$subA_present <- df$DYGY_SubA > 0
df$subA_specific <- df$subA_present & df$other_zero_A

subA_specific_families <- sum(df$subA_specific)
subA_specific_genes <- sum(df$DYGY_SubA[df$subA_specific])

# ---- DYGY_SubB 特有 ----
df$other_zero_B <- apply(df[, other_species], 1, function(x) all(x == 0))
df$subB_present <- df$DYGY_SubB > 0
df$subB_specific <- df$subB_present & df$other_zero_B

subB_specific_families <- sum(df$subB_specific)
subB_specific_genes <- sum(df$DYGY_SubB[df$subB_specific])

# ---- 所有物种共有基因家族 ----
df$core_og <- apply(df[, species_cols], 1, function(x) all(x > 0))

core_OG_families <- sum(df$core_og)
core_OG_genes <- sum(df[df$core_og, species_cols])

# ---- 总基因家族和总基因数 ----
total_OGs <- nrow(df)
total_genes <- sum(df[, species_cols])

# ---- 输出结果 ----
cat("=== DYGY 特有基因统计（分亚基因组） ===\n")
cat("DYGY_SubA 特有基因家族数 =", subA_specific_families, "\n")
cat("DYGY_SubA 特有基因总数 =", subA_specific_genes, "\n\n")
cat("DYGY_SubB 特有基因家族数 =", subB_specific_families, "\n")
cat("DYGY_SubB 特有基因总数 =", subB_specific_genes, "\n\n")

cat("=== 所有物种共有基因统计 ===\n")
cat("所有物种共有基因家族数 =", core_OG_families, "\n")
cat("所有物种共有基因总数 =", core_OG_genes, "\n\n")

cat("=== 总基因家族和总基因数 ===\n")
cat("总基因家族数 =", total_OGs, "\n")
cat("总基因数 =", total_genes, "\n")

=== DYGY 特有基因统计（分亚基因组） ===
DYGY_SubA 特有基因家族数 = 3793
DYGY_SubA 特有基因总数 = 10060

DYGY_SubB 特有基因家族数 = 3122
DYGY_SubB 特有基因总数 = 6723

=== 所有物种共有基因统计 ===
所有物种共有基因家族数 = 6272
所有物种共有基因总数 = 137694

=== 总基因家族和总基因数 ===
总基因家族数 = 27884
总基因数 = 383493
```

```bash
#/data/xueqin/Prunus/genome_assembly/1Chromosome/06_polish/18.Subphaser/orthofinder3/1orthofinder_2025.12.10_thaliana_11_subA_B/Prunus_conradiae_2025.12.18_new/OrthoFinder/Results_Dec18/Single_Copy_Orthologue_Sequences1/Unique_shared_genes_2025.8.8
library(ggplot2)
library(readr)
library(dplyr)
library(tidyr)
# 读取TSV数据
df <- read.table("Orthogroups.GeneCount.tsv", header = TRUE, sep = "\t", check.names = FALSE)
head(df)
# 去除不需要的列
df$Total <- NULL  # 如果有Total列
orthogroups <- df$Orthogroup
df <- df[, -1]

df_long <- df %>%
  mutate(Orthogroup = orthogroups) %>%
  pivot_longer(-Orthogroup, names_to = "Species", values_to = "CopyNumber")

# 分组为五类
df_long <- df_long %>%
  mutate(CopyCategory = case_when(
    CopyNumber == 1 ~ "1",
    CopyNumber == 2 ~ "2",
    CopyNumber == 3 ~ "3",
    CopyNumber == 4 ~ "4",
    CopyNumber > 4  ~ "4+",
    CopyNumber == 0 ~ NA_character_
  )) %>%
  filter(!is.na(CopyCategory))  # 去掉 CopyNumber == 0 的情况

# 统计每个物种中各类拷贝数的基因数
df_summary <- df_long %>%
  group_by(Species, CopyCategory) %>%
  summarise(Count = n(), .groups = "drop")

# 保持拷贝类别顺序
df_summary$CopyCategory <- factor(df_summary$CopyCategory,
                                  levels = c("1", "2", "3", "4", "4+"))

# 统计每个物种中各类拷贝数的基因数
df_summary <- df_long %>%
  group_by(Species, CopyCategory) %>%
  summarise(Count = n(), .groups = "drop")

# ---- 新增：计算每个物种内部 CopyCategory 的占比 ----
df_summary <- df_summary %>%
  group_by(Species) %>%
  mutate(Total = sum(Count),
         Percentage = Count / Total) %>%
  ungroup()
df_summary
# A tibble: 55 × 5
   Species              CopyCategory Count Total Percentage
   <chr>                <chr>        <int> <int>      <dbl>
 1 Amborella_trichopoda 1            10762 14353     0.750
 2 Amborella_trichopoda 2             2145 14353     0.149
 3 Amborella_trichopoda 3              564 14353     0.0393
 4 Amborella_trichopoda 4              255 14353     0.0178
 5 Amborella_trichopoda 4+             627 14353     0.0437
 6 Arabidopsis_thaliana 1             5771 13430     0.430
 7 Arabidopsis_thaliana 2             3736 13430     0.278
 8 Arabidopsis_thaliana 3             1791 13430     0.133
 9 Arabidopsis_thaliana 4              903 13430     0.0672
10 Arabidopsis_thaliana 4+            1229 13430     0.0915
# ℹ 45 more rows
# ℹ Use `print(n = ...)` to see more rows
write.table(df_summary,
            file = "Copy_number_percentage_summary.tsv",
            sep = "\t",
            quote = FALSE,
            row.names = FALSE)


pdf("Copy number percentage of all gene families.pdf", height = 6, width = 10)

ggplot(df_summary, aes(x = Percentage, y = Species, fill = CopyCategory)) +
  geom_bar(stat = "identity", position = "fill", width = 0.5) + 
  scale_fill_manual(values = c(
    "1" = "#CD5C5C",
    "2" = "#354898",
    "3" = "#73ABCF",
    "4" = "#911D36",
    "4+" = "#FAAE5F"
  )) +
  labs(x = "Proportion of Gene Families", 
       y = "Species", 
       fill = "Copy Number") +
  scale_x_continuous(labels = scales::percent_format(accuracy = 1)) +  # 显示成百分比
  theme_minimal(base_size = 12) +
  theme(
    axis.title.x = element_text(size = 16),
    axis.title.y = element_text(size = 16),
    axis.text.y = element_text(size = 12),
    axis.text.x = element_text(size = 12),
    axis.line.x = element_line(color = "black"),
    axis.line.y = element_line(color = "black"),
    panel.grid = element_blank(),
    panel.background = element_blank(),
    axis.ticks = element_line(color = "black"),
    legend.text = element_text(size = 12),
    legend.title = element_text(size = 14),
  )

dev.off()


```

###### 基因家族收缩和扩张分析
路径：/data/xueqin/Prunus/genome_assembly/1Chromosome/06_polish/18.Subphaser/orthofinder3/1orthofinder_2025.12.10_thaliana_11_subA_B/Prunus_conradiae_2025.12.18_new/OrthoFinder/Results_Dec18/Single_Copy_Orthologue_Sequences1/CAFE_2025.12.20/k3p

```bash
#运行cafe5
cafe5 -i gene_families_filter_final.txt -t Prunus.nw.ultra.tre -p -k 2 -o k2p
cafe5 -i gene_families_filter_final.txt -t Prunus.nw.ultra.tre -p -k 3 -o k3p
cafe5 -i gene_families_filter_final.txt -t Prunus.nw.ultra.tre -p -k 4 -o k4p
cafe5 -i gene_families_filter_final.txt -t Prunus.nw.ultra.tre -p -k 5 -o k5p
cafe5 -i gene_families_filter_final.txt -t Prunus.nw.ultra.tre -p -k 6 -o k6p

#CAFE5的结果
model_results.txt
#模型，最终似然值，最终Lambda值等参数信息。
选择最优结果
#k值的结果比较：选择k4p结果最佳
#查看k2p，k3p，k5p，k6p等不同的结果文件Gamma_results.txt文件中的第一行信息，Model Gamma Final Likelihood (-lnL)值，挑选最大的为最优结果。
k2p：Model Gamma Final Likelihood (-lnL): 169677
k3p：Model Gamma Final Likelihood (-lnL): 169669
k4p：Model Gamma Final Likelihood (-lnL): 180795
k5p：Model Gamma Final Likelihood (-lnL): 170957
k6p：Model Gamma Final Likelihood (-lnL): 174395

#结果整理
cd k4p
mkdir Prunus_zippeliana
cd Prunus_zippeliana
cp ../Gamma_change.tab ../Gamma_asr.tre ../Gamma_family_results.txt ./
#（1）对特定物种显著扩张或显著收缩基因的提取
#查看自己想获取的物种是第几列
head -1 Gamma_change.tab | tr '\t' '\n' | nl -v 0 | grep "Prunus_zippeliana"
# 3  Prunus_zippeliana<3>

# 提取Gamma_change.tab第4列（Prunus_zippeliana）扩张的orthogroupsID
awk -F"\t" '$4 > 0 {print $1, $4}' Gamma_change.tab > Prunus_zippeliana.expanded

# 提取Gamma_change.tab第6列（Prunus_zippeliana）收缩的orthogroupsID
awk -F"\t" '$4 < 0 {print $1, $4}' Gamma_change.tab > Prunus_zippeliana.contracted

# 根据Prunus_zippeliana ID和编号提取其分支的显著扩张或收缩的基因家族树（p<0.05）
grep "Prunus_zippeliana<3>\*" Gamma_asr.tre > Prunus_zippeliana_significant_trees.tre

# 提取Prunus_zippeliana分支显著变化的OG IDs
grep -E -o "OG[0-9]+" Prunus_zippeliana_significant_trees.tre > Prunus_zippeliana_significant.ogs

# 以p<0.01为标准提取所有显著扩张或收缩的orthogroupsID
awk '$2 < 0.01 {print $1}' Gamma_family_results.txt > p0.01_significant.ogs

# 提取p<0.01显著性的Prunus_zippeliana分支变化的OG IDs
grep -f Prunus_zippeliana_significant.ogs p0.01_significant.ogs > Prunus_zippeliana_p0.01_significant.ogs

# 提取显著扩张的Prunus_zippeliana物种的orthogroupsID
grep -f Prunus_zippeliana_p0.01_significant.ogs Prunus_zippeliana.expanded | cut -f1 > Prunus_zippeliana.expanded.significant

# 提取显著收缩的Prunus_zippeliana物种的orthogroupsID
grep -f Prunus_zippeliana_p0.01_significant.ogs Prunus_zippeliana.contracted | cut -f1 > Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant

# 提取显著扩张的基因列表（假设基因ID前缀为P.zippeliana.）
cp ../../../../Orthogroups/Orthogroups.txt ./
cut -f1 -d' ' Prunus_zippeliana.expanded.significant | \
grep -F -f - Orthogroups.txt | \
sed 's/[ \t]/\n/g' | grep "P.zippeliana." | sort | uniq > Prunus_zippeliana.expanded.significant.genes

# 提取显著收缩的基因列表（假设基因ID前缀为bv）
cut -f1 -d' ' Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant  | \
grep -F -f - Orthogroups.txt | \
sed 's/[ \t]/\n/g' | grep "P.zippeliana." | sort | uniq > Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant.genes

cp ../../../../../../Prunus_zippeliana.fa ./
# 提取显著扩张的基因序列
conda activate base
seqkit grep -f Prunus_zippeliana.expanded.significant.genes Prunus_zippeliana.fa > Prunus_zippeliana.expanded.significant.pep.fa

# 提取显著收缩的基因序列
seqkit grep -f Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant.genes Prunus_zippeliana.fa > Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant.pep.fa

#（2）提取出指定物种的显著扩张和收缩的蛋白序列之后，就可以拿去做GO注释和基因富集分析。
cp Prunus_zippeliana.expanded.significant.pep.fa expanded.significant.pep.fa
cp Prunus_zippeliana.contracted.significant.pep.fa contracted.significant.pep.fa
gzip contracted.significant.pep.fa
gzip expanded.significant.pep.fa

cd ../ #5.把每个节点收缩扩张的基因数量画在树上 #绘制基因家族扩张数量的图片： python3 /data/xueqin/software/CAFE5/docs/tutorial/cafe5_draw_tree.py \   -i Gamma_clade_results.txt \   -d Gamma_report.cafe \   -y Increase \   -o expansion_tree.pdf    #绘制基因家族收缩数量的图片： python3 /data/xueqin/software/CAFE5/docs/tutorial/cafe5_draw_tree.py \   -i Gamma_clade_results.txt \   -d Gamma_report.cafe \   -y Decrease \   -o contraction_tree.pdf 
 #整理进化树格式画图即可 awk '/TREE \S+ =/{print $NF}' Gamma_asr.tre | sed -r 's/_[0-9]+//g' | sed -r 's/([^)])<[0-9]+>/\1/g' | sed 's/*//g' > out_ortho.tree #最后上传到ITOL美化即可
```
